-- ============================================================
-- SCHIZHUBV6 - THE HOOD CLASSIC, REBUILT
-- Layered rebuild: A config -> B theme -> C UI factory -> D shell
-- -> E pages -> F feature modules -> G movement -> H wiring
-- V6 delta vs V4:
--   * build variants: "clean" (Schizhubv6.txt, default) and
--     "glass" (Schizhubv6_DGB.txt, dark-glass translucency) --
--     set BUILD_VARIANT below, everything else derives from it
--   * no more JSON / external-file saving anywhere: ESP tools and
--     the movement settings now save in-session only, and the new
--     UI PRESETS section (Settings) bookmarks whole looks (theme /
--     size / font / border / edging) into slots you can switch
--     between; Copy emits a snippet to paste back into the script
--     (IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS) for permanence -- one script, one hub
--   * Server sub-tab rebuilt as one scrollable page with grouped
--     sections (Movement / Toggles / Boosts) -- fixes the v4 row
--     collision where the movement toggles overlapped NOCLIP/JERK
--   * bugfix pass: noclip connection leak, follow/chase never
--     restored WalkSpeed, fling left FallenPartsDestroyHeight NaN,
--     tracers used a camera lookup that is nil in modern Roblox,
--     player picker could open off-screen, empty commands printed
--     "Unknown command: nil", extras tab reset its sub-tab, and
--     the double-encoded UTF-8 (mojibake) strings were re-encoded
--   * design overhaul: accent-ticked section headers, hover states
--     on buttons, monogram title chip + underline, modern toast
-- Tracker fixes 1-7 carried over; harness-stub switches left as-is.
-- ============================================================
-- BUILD VARIANT: "clean" = Schizhubv6.txt, "glass" = Schizhubv6_DGB.txt

local function bindThemed(obj, property, key)
    obj[property] = type(key) == "function" and key() or COLOR[key]
    table.insert(themedElements, {instance = obj, property = property, key = key})
end

-- COLOR PALETTE (shared by all color pickers)

local ColorPalette = {
    { name = "Red",    value = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70) },
    { name = "Orange", value = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 50) },
    { name = "Yellow", value = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60) },
    { name = "Green",  value = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 120) },
    { name = "Cyan",   value = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 220) },
    { name = "Blue",   value = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235) },
    { name = "Violet", value = Color3.fromRGB(170, 120, 230) },
    { name = "Rose",   value = Color3.fromRGB(240, 120, 160) },
    { name = "Indigo", value = Color3.fromRGB(100, 110, 230) },
    { name = "Slate",  value = Color3.fromRGB(120, 130, 150) },
    { name = "White",  value = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230) },
    { name = "Black",  value = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18) },
    { name = "Dark",   value = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 27) },
    { name = "Char",   value = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34) },
}
local function PaletteRgb(col)
    return string.format("%d, %d, %d", math.round(col.R * 255), math.round(col.G * 255), math.round(col.B * 255))
end
local DEFAULT_COLOR_NAMES = {
    [COLOR.accent] = "Blue",
    [COLOR.accent2] = "Purple",
    [COLOR.accent3] = "Amber",
    [COLOR.border] = "Gray",
    [COLOR.bg] = "Dark",
}
local function ColorPaletteName(col)
    for _, o in ipairs(ColorPalette) do
        if o.value == col then return o.name end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(THEME_PRESETS) do
        if p.accent == col or p.accent2 == col or p.accent3 == col then return p.name end
    end
    if col then return DEFAULT_COLOR_NAMES[col] or PaletteRgb(col) end
    return "Default"
end

-- ============================================================
-- THEME APPLY + RECOLOR SWEEP
-- ============================================================

-- Background/text compatibility sweep: any element still painted with the
-- previous gray/text tones gets moved to the new ones, so a custom background
-- or text color recolors the whole already-built window in one pass. The
-- previous tone set is parked on COLOR.__prev (a field, not a new local).
-- V6: the sweep also applies the Dark Glass translucency (BUILD_VARIANT ==
-- "glass") to every role-colored surface, so panels built at any time --
-- including floating panels opened later -- keep the glass look on theme
-- switches. recolorTree() is exposed at chunk scope so late-built panels
-- can be re-tinted on open.
local function recolorTree()
    local stack = {HUB.frame}
    while #stack > 0 do
        local obj = table.remove(stack)
        -- Guarded reads: not every instance type exposes BackgroundColor3
        -- or TextColor3 (UIStroke, UIListLayout, UIScale, ...), and Roblox
        -- throws on invalid member access, which would kill the whole
        -- recolor pass. Only match objects that actually have the member.
        local okB, bc = pcall(function() return obj.BackgroundColor3 end)
        if okB and bc then
            local prev = COLOR.__prev
            local finalBc = bc
            if prev then
                if bc == prev.panel then finalBc = COLOR.panel
                elseif bc == prev.playerBtn then finalBc = COLOR.playerBtn
                elseif bc == prev.switchOff then finalBc = COLOR.switchOff
                elseif bc == prev.bg then finalBc = COLOR.bg
                elseif bc == prev.titleBar then finalBc = COLOR.titleBar
                end
                if finalBc ~= bc then obj.BackgroundColor3 = finalBc end
            end
            if BUILD_VARIANT == "glass" then
                local tint = (finalBc == COLOR.bg and 0.30)
                    or (finalBc == COLOR.panel and 0.32)
                    or (finalBc == COLOR.playerBtn and 0.42)
                    or (finalBc == COLOR.switchOff and 0.55)
                    or (finalBc == COLOR.titleBar and 0.26)
                    or nil
                if tint then obj.BackgroundTransparency = tint end
            end
        end
        local okT, tc = pcall(function() return obj.TextColor3 end)
        if okT and tc and prev then
            if tc == prev.text then obj.TextColor3 = COLOR.text
            elseif tc == prev.textDim then obj.TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetChildren()) do table.insert(stack, child) end
    end
end
local MARK_TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
local function refreshTheme()
    if HUB.frame then recolorTree() end
    for _, e in ipairs(themedElements) do
        if e.instance and e.instance.Parent then
            e.instance[e.property] = type(e.key) == "function" and e.key() or COLOR[e.key]
        end
    end
    for _, t in ipairs(switchTracks) do
        if t.track.Parent then
            t.track.BackgroundColor3 = t.get() and COLOR.accent or COLOR.switchOff
            if t.mark then t.mark.TextColor3 = MARK_TEXT_COLOR end
        end
    end
    COLOR.__prev = {
        bg = COLOR.bg, panel = COLOR.panel, playerBtn = COLOR.playerBtn,
        switchOff = COLOR.switchOff, titleBar = COLOR.titleBar,
        text = COLOR.text, textDim = COLOR.textDim,
    }
end
local currentPreset = THEME_PRESETS[1]
local defaultBorder = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 48)
-- V6: live "current" selections, tracked so the UI PRESETS bookmarks
-- (Settings) can capture and restore the whole look in one shot.
local currentSizePreset = SIZE_PRESETS[3]
HUB.currentTextPreset = HUB.TEXT_SCALE_PRESETS[3]
local currentBorderPreset = BORDER_EDGING_PRESETS[2]
local currentEdgingFilter = nil
for _, f in ipairs(EDGING_FILTERS) do
    if f.mode == "bg" then currentEdgingFilter = f break end
end

-- Lighten (positive factor) or darken (negative factor) a color toward the
-- theme's light/dark variants.
local function shadeColor(col, factor)
    local r, g, b = col.R * 255, col.G * 255, col.B * 255
    if factor >= 0 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(r + (255 - r) * factor, g + (255 - g) * factor, b + (255 - b) * factor)
    end
    return Color3.fromRGB(r * (1 + factor), g * (1 + factor), b * (1 + factor))
end
-- Background compatibility: panels, player buttons, switch tracks and the
-- header bar all derive from the background (a fixed per-channel shift toward
-- light or dark), so a custom background never leaves the buttons looking odd.
-- The sweep that repaints already-built elements lives in refreshTheme().
local function deriveBackground(bg)
    COLOR.bg = bg
    COLOR.panel = Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(bg.R * 255 + 6, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.G * 255 + 6, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.B * 255 + 7, 0, 255))
    COLOR.playerBtn = Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(bg.R * 255 + 12, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.G * 255 + 12, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.B * 255 + 13, 0, 255))
    COLOR.switchOff = Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(bg.R * 255 + 26, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.G * 255 + 26, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.B * 255 + 28, 0, 255))
    COLOR.titleBar = Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(bg.R * 255 - 4, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.G * 255 - 4, 0, 255), math.clamp(bg.B * 255 - 4, 0, 255))
end
local function deriveText(text)
    COLOR.text = text
    COLOR.textDim = Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(text.R * 255 - 85, 0, 255), math.clamp(text.G * 255 - 85, 0, 255), math.clamp(text.B * 255 - 85, 0, 255))
end
-- All display theming is preset-based (no custom color slot): applying a theme
-- just swaps the accent family and derives the background/text compatibility
-- tones, then repaints everything already built.
local function applyTheme(preset)
    currentPreset = preset
    COLOR.accent, COLOR.accent2, COLOR.accent3 = preset.accent, preset.accent2, preset.accent3
    COLOR.accentLight, COLOR.accentDark = shadeColor(preset.accent, 0.4), shadeColor(preset.accent, -0.4)
    COLOR.accent2Light, COLOR.accent2Dark = shadeColor(preset.accent2, 0.4), shadeColor(preset.accent2, -0.4)
    COLOR.accent3Light, COLOR.accent3Dark = shadeColor(preset.accent3, 0.4), shadeColor(preset.accent3, -0.4)
    if preset.bg then deriveBackground(preset.bg) end
    if preset.text then deriveText(preset.text) end
    refreshTheme()
end
applyTheme(THEME_PRESETS[1])

-- ============================================================
-- FONT THEMING
-- ============================================================
local themedFonts = {}
local currentFontPreset = FONT_PRESETS[1]
-- [FIX 2] Normalize the recorded base at bind time so objects bound before
-- their TextSize is meaningful can't poison applyTextScale.
-- [FIX 11] Guard against non-text instances: reading TextSize on a Frame
-- throws in real Roblox, so skip anything that is not a text object.
local function bindFont(obj, role)
    if not (obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextBox")) then
        return
    end
    table.insert(themedFonts, {instance = obj, role = role, base = math.max(8, obj.TextSize or 12)})
end
local function applyFont(preset)
    currentFontPreset = preset
    for _, e in ipairs(themedFonts) do
        if e.instance and e.instance.Parent then
            e.instance.Font = (e.role == "bold") and preset.bold or preset.regular
        end
    end
end

local hubUIScale
-- Small text bump that follows the hub-size presets: every bound label's base
-- size is rescaled by the preset's text factor, so bigger hubs read naturally.
local function applyTextScale(factor)
    for _, e in ipairs(themedFonts) do
        if e.instance and e.instance.Parent then
            e.instance.TextSize = math.max(8, math.floor((e.base or 12) * factor))
        end
    end
end
local function applySize(preset)
    currentSizePreset = preset
    TweenService:Create(hubUIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Scale = preset.scale}):Play()
    applyTextScale((preset.text or 1) * (HUB.currentTextPreset and HUB.currentTextPreset.factor or 1))
end
-- REDESIGN: standalone text-size presets -- rescale all bound labels by
-- the text preset, keeping the hub size preset's own text bump folded in.
local function applyTextPreset(preset)
    HUB.currentTextPreset = preset
    applyTextScale((currentSizePreset.text or 1) * preset.factor)
end
-- ============================================================
-- PART C. GUI FACTORY + OVERLAYS
-- ============================================================
local function newInstance(className, props)
    local obj = Instance.new(className)
    for k, v in pairs(props) do obj[k] = v end
    return obj
end

-- Both helpers now return the created instance so the window shell can keep
-- handles on them for live border/corner restyling.
local function round(obj, radius)
    local corner = newInstance("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8), Parent = obj})
    return corner
end

local function stroke(obj, color, thickness)
    local edge = newInstance("UIStroke", {Color = color or COLOR.border, Thickness = thickness or 1, Parent = obj})
    return edge
end

HUB.sectionHeaders = HUB.sectionHeaders or {}

-- Original section header: accent tick + centered label, no collapse.
-- Used by the fixed pages (TP view / Chase / Ply Handler) exactly like
-- the base build.
local function sectionHeader(parent, text, y)
    local row = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, y),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = parent,
    })
    local tick = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 10), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -5),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent2, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = row,
    })
    round(tick, 1.5)
    local label = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Parent = row,
    })
    bindFont(label, "bold")
    return row, label
end

-- REDESIGN [SMART REFLOW]: click-to-collapse header for the scrolling
-- pages (Server sub-tab, Settings, ESP/SPD/CNSL). Collapsing hides the
-- section's content AND shifts every section below it up so nothing
-- floats under the collapsed header; the page canvas (and the extras
-- window height) refit to the remaining content. Expanding reverses it.
-- Each header tracks a CURRENT y (curY) because collapsing earlier
-- sections physically moves later ones -- build-time y values stay put
-- in the registry and are only used for ordering + span math.
-- [POLISH] The content of every section is SNAPSHOTTED on first toggle
-- (the direct children between this header and the next, plus their
-- offset from the header). Expanding re-places those widgets at
-- header + offset -- never by searching for old positions -- and
-- shiftBelow skips hidden children entirely, so a collapsed section's
-- invisible content can never shove visible content down/off-screen or
-- collide with sections below it after nested collapse/expand chains.
HUB.contentBottom = {}
HUB.collapsibleHeader = function(parent, text, y, shiftParent)
    shiftParent = shiftParent or parent
    local row = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, y),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = parent,
    })
    local tick = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 10), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -5),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent2, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = row,
    })
    round(tick, 1.5)
    local label = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Parent = row,
    })
    bindFont(label, "bold")
    -- [POLISH] right-side chevron: shows the section is collapsible and
    -- flips ▾/▸ with the collapse state (hidden row when collapsed so the
    -- header reads as flat).
    local chevron = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, -5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "▾", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Parent = row,
    })
    bindFont(chevron, "bold")
    local clickBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = row,
    })
    local entry = { parent = parent, y = y, curY = y, collapsed = false, contentH = nil, items = nil, finalized = false }
    -- Order by CURRENT y (curY), not the build-time y: collapsing earlier
    -- sections physically moves later ones, and the AIM/OVERLAYS blocks are
    -- shifted down after the server build, so build-y order no longer
    -- matches the on-screen order. Build y stays only for span math below.
    local function nextHeaderEntry()
        local best, bestCur = nil, math.huge
        for _, e in ipairs(HUB.sectionHeaders) do
            if e.parent == parent and e.curY and e.curY > entry.curY and e.curY < bestCur then
                best, bestCur = e, e.curY
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    -- [FIX] Snapshot this section's content exactly ONCE, from the INITIAL
    -- layout: HUB.finalizeCollapsibles runs after the whole script builds
    -- (every header + its content + contentBottom exists by then), and
    -- toggle() falls back to it on the first toggle as a safety net. Items
    -- are the direct children sitting between this header and the next one,
    -- recorded as an offset from the header so an expand can always drop
    -- them exactly under the header no matter how other sections have
    -- shifted since. The snapshot MUST come from the un-shifted layout:
    -- capturing from a shifted position let hidden content of a collapsed
    -- section above leak into the section below (it keeps its old y while
    -- the next header moved up), and made the LAST section swallow every
    -- child in the page after a collapse-all (its curY stacks near the top
    -- while contentBottom still reflects the page bottom). Once captured,
    -- items/off/contentH are constants -- they are shift-invariant -- so
    -- every later collapse/expand stays consistent.
    local function finalizeSection()
        if entry.finalized then return end
        entry.finalized = true
        local nextE = nextHeaderEntry()
        local bottom = nextE and nextE.curY or (HUB.contentBottom[parent] or (entry.curY + 20))
        entry.contentH = math.max(0, bottom - entry.curY - 14)
        entry.items = {}
        for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
            if child ~= row then
                local cy = child.Position and child.Position.Y and child.Position.Y.Offset
                if cy and cy > entry.curY and cy < bottom then
                    entry.items[#entry.items + 1] = { obj = child, off = cy - entry.curY }
                end
            end
        end
    end
    entry.finalize = finalizeSection
    -- Moves only VISIBLE children (and the section headers below fromY) by
    -- delta. Hidden content of a collapsed section is skipped -- it occupies
    -- no space, so it must never shove visible content around; it is dropped
    -- back under its own header when that section expands.
    local function shiftBelow(delta, fromY)
        if delta == 0 then return end
        fromY = fromY or (entry.curY + 14)
        for _, child in ipairs(shiftParent:GetChildren()) do
            if child ~= row and child.Visible ~= false and child.Position then
                local py = child.Position.Y
                local cy = py and py.Offset
                if cy and cy >= fromY then
                    child.Position = UDim2.new(child.Position.X.Scale, child.Position.X.Offset, child.Position.Y.Scale, cy + delta)
                end
            end
        end
        for _, e in ipairs(HUB.sectionHeaders) do
            if (e.parent == shiftParent or e.parent == parent) and e.curY and e.curY >= fromY then
                e.curY = e.curY + delta
            end
        end
    end
    local function refit()
        HUB.fitExtrasWindow(shiftParent)
    end
    local function toggle()
        entry.collapsed = not entry.collapsed
        label.TextColor3 = entry.collapsed and COLOR.off or COLOR.textDim
        tick.Visible = not entry.collapsed
        chevron.Text = entry.collapsed and "▸" or "▾"
        chevron.TextColor3 = entry.collapsed and COLOR.off or COLOR.textDim
        local nextE = nextHeaderEntry()
        local nextCurY = nextE and nextE.curY or (HUB.contentBottom[parent] or (entry.curY + 20))
        finalizeSection()
        local contentH = entry.contentH
        local items = entry.items
        if entry.collapsed then
            for _, it in ipairs(items) do it.obj.Visible = false end
            shiftBelow(-contentH, nextCurY)
        else
            -- Shift the sections below FIRST, then drop this section's
            -- content back under its own header. Placing first then shifting
            -- would move the just-placed rows a second time -- they sit at or
            -- above the next header's collapsed position -- and jumble them
            -- over the section below (Movement overlapping V7 Movement).
            shiftBelow(contentH, nextCurY)
            for _, it in ipairs(items) do
                it.obj.Position = UDim2.new(it.obj.Position.X.Scale, it.obj.Position.X.Offset, it.obj.Position.Y.Scale, entry.curY + it.off)
                it.obj.Visible = true
            end
        end
        if shiftParent ~= parent then
            parent.Size = UDim2.new(parent.Size.X.Scale, parent.Size.X.Offset, 0,
                entry.collapsed and 18 or (contentH + 14))
        end
        refit()
    end
    entry.toggle = toggle
    clickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggle)
    clickBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() label.TextColor3 = COLOR.text chevron.TextColor3 = COLOR.text end)
    clickBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not entry.collapsed then
            label.TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim
            chevron.TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim
        end
    end)
    table.insert(HUB.sectionHeaders, entry)
    return row, label
end

-- [FIX] Capture every section's content from the finished, un-shifted
-- layout. Must run AFTER the whole script builds (all collapsibleHeader
-- calls, all content, and every contentBottom set). Called once at the end
-- of the script; toggle() falls back to finalizeSection() if an entry
-- somehow missed it.
HUB.finalizeCollapsibles = function()
    for _, e in ipairs(HUB.sectionHeaders) do
        if e.finalize then e.finalize() end
    end
end

-- Shared extras-page fit: recompute the live content bottom, keep the
-- canvas in sync, and -- only when the view IS the active sub-tab -- size
-- the WINDOW to that content (capped at the screen) while sizing the VIEW
-- to the window, so taller content scrolls INSIDE the window instead of
-- spilling past its bottom edge at larger hub sizes. Also used on sub-tab
-- switch (showExtrasSubTab), not only on header toggle, so the Server tab
-- fits itself the moment it is shown without needing a header click.
HUB.fitExtrasWindow = function(view)
    local bottom = 0
    for _, child in ipairs(view:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Visible ~= false and child.Position then
            local py = child.Position.Y
            local cy = py and py.Offset
            local sy = child.Size and child.Size.Y
            local ch = sy and sy.Offset or 0
            if cy and cy + ch > bottom then bottom = cy + ch end
        end
    end
    HUB.contentBottom[view] = bottom
    if view:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
        view.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(bottom + 8, 1))
    end
    if HUB.currentPage == "extras" then
        local sub = HUB.extrasSubTab
        local active = (sub == "ESP" and HUB.views.esp) or (sub == "SPD" and HUB.views.spd)
            or (sub == "CNSL" and HUB.views.console) or (sub == "TP" and HUB.views.tp)
            or HUB.views.server
        if view == active then
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vpY = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
            local winH = math.min(83 + bottom + 8, vpY - 40)
            HUB.frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, HUB.winW, 0, winH)
            view.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, math.max(winH - 83, 40))
        end
    end
end

local function attachHover(btn, role)
    local function restColor()
        if role then return COLOR[role] end
        return COLOR.panel
    end
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(restColor(), -0.16)
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = restColor()
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- ROOT / OVERLAY LAYER
-- ============================================================
HUB.screenGui = newInstance("ScreenGui", {Name = "PHHub", Parent = game.CoreGui, ResetOnSpawn = false})
HUB.overlayLayer = newInstance("Frame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 50, Parent = HUB.screenGui,
})

local activeOverlay = nil
local function closeActiveOverlay()
    if activeOverlay then activeOverlay:Destroy() activeOverlay = nil end
end

-- Shared registry for the EXTERNAL side UIs (the ≡ flyout and the bell
-- notification panel). Only one may be open at a time: opening one
-- closes the others first.
HUB.externalUI = { panels = {} }
function HUB.externalUI.register(name, closeFn)
    HUB.externalUI.panels[name] = closeFn
end
function HUB.externalUI.open(name)
    for n, fn in pairs(HUB.externalUI.panels) do
        if n ~= name then fn() end
    end
end

-- Shared HOVER manager for the external panels (the ≡ flyout and the
-- notification center). Both buttons sit on the window's right edge and
-- both panels open OUTSIDE the window, to the right of their button, so
-- one implementation keeps their hover behavior identical. A tiny open
-- delay stops accidental pops when the mouse sweeps across the tabs; a
-- close grace lets the mouse travel from the button onto the panel.
--   button  right-edge tab     panel  the pop-out frame
--   name    HUB.externalUI key ("flyout"/"notify")
--   onOpen  called when the panel opens, before it turns visible
--   baseCol button color while closed (accent while open)
local function wireExternalHover(button, panel, name, onOpen, baseCol)
    local open = false
    local openJob, closeJob = nil, nil
    local function cancelJobs()
        if openJob then task.cancel(openJob) openJob = nil end
        if closeJob then task.cancel(closeJob) closeJob = nil end
    end
    local function setOpen(on)
        if open == on then return end
        open = on
        if on then
            onOpen()
            button.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent
        else
            button.BackgroundColor3 = baseCol
        end
        panel.Visible = on
    end
    local function scheduleOpen()
        cancelJobs()
        openJob = task.delay(0.08, function() openJob = nil setOpen(true) end)
    end
    local function scheduleClose()
        cancelJobs()
        closeJob = task.delay(0.35, function() closeJob = nil setOpen(false) end)
    end
    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not open then button.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(baseCol, -0.16) end
        scheduleOpen()
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not open then button.BackgroundColor3 = baseCol end
        scheduleClose()
    end)
    panel.MouseEnter:Connect(cancelJobs)
    panel.MouseLeave:Connect(scheduleClose)
    HUB.externalUI.register(name, function() cancelJobs() setOpen(false) end)
    return function() cancelJobs() setOpen(false) end
end

-- ============================================================
-- TOAST NOTIFICATIONS (defined here because createColorPicker and
-- the command engine both use showToast; the label is built lazily
-- on first use so Part C does not depend on HUB.frame (Part D)).
-- REDESIGN: a pool of stacked toasts so messages queue instead of
-- stomping each other. REDESIGN [NOTIFY CONSOLE]: pool size, hold
-- time and anchor come from HUB.notifyConfig (tweakable in the
-- notification console), and every toast is logged to a history
-- table shown by the side notification tab. Everything but showToast
-- lives in the do-block below so it stays under the 200-local cap.
-- ============================================================
local showToast
do
local toastToken = 0
HUB.toastPool = {}
HUB.toastHistory = {}
HUB.notifyConfig = {
    duration = 7.5,      -- seconds a toast stays up
    maxSlots = 3,        -- concurrent toasts
    anchor = "bottom-left",
    historyCap = 12,
    -- Where notifications surface: "center" logs to the external center
    -- only, "legacy" also shows the old floating in-hub toasts, and
    -- "terminal" makes the bell rise and drops a CMD-style readout.
    mode = "center",
}
local TOAST_ANCHORS = {
    ["top-left"]      = { anchor = Vector2.new(0, 0),   base = UDim2.new(0, 8,   0, 8) },
    ["top-center"]    = { anchor = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), base = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8) },
    ["top-right"]     = { anchor = Vector2.new(1, 0),   base = UDim2.new(1, -8,  0, 8) },
    ["bottom-left"]   = { anchor = Vector2.new(0, 1),   base = UDim2.new(0, 8,   1, -34) },
    ["bottom-center"] = { anchor = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), base = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -34) },
    ["bottom-right"]  = { anchor = Vector2.new(1, 1),   base = UDim2.new(1, -8,  1, -34) },
}
local function toastSlotPosition(i)
    local a = TOAST_ANCHORS[HUB.notifyConfig.anchor] or TOAST_ANCHORS["bottom-left"]
    local base = a.base
    local y = base.Y.Offset + (i - 1) * 24
    if a.anchor.Y > 0 then y = base.Y.Offset - (i - 1) * 24 end
    return UDim2.new(base.X.Scale, base.X.Offset, base.Y.Scale, y), a.anchor
end
local function repositionToastPool()
    for i, slot in ipairs(HUB.toastPool) do
        local p, anchor = toastSlotPosition(i)
        slot.label.Position = p
        slot.label.AnchorPoint = anchor
    end
end
HUB.repositionToastPool = repositionToastPool
local function ensureToastPool()
    local need = math.clamp(HUB.notifyConfig.maxSlots, 1, 4)
    while #HUB.toastPool > need do
        local slot = table.remove(HUB.toastPool)
        if slot.job then task.cancel(slot.job) slot.job = nil end
        if slot.label then slot.label:Destroy() end
    end
    for i = #HUB.toastPool + 1, need do
        local t = newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 20),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -34 - (i - 1) * 24),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.titleBar,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            TextTransparency = 1,
            TextColor3 = COLOR.warn,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            Parent = (HUB.frame or HUB.screenGui),
            ZIndex = 40,
            Visible = false,
        })
        round(t, 9)
        local edge = stroke(t, COLOR.border, 1)
        bindThemed(t, "BackgroundColor3", "titleBar")
        bindThemed(edge, "Color", "border")
        bindFont(t, "bold")
        HUB.toastPool[i] = { label = t, edge = edge, job = nil, token = 0 }
    end
    repositionToastPool()
end
HUB.ensureToastPool = ensureToastPool
showToast = function(text, colorKey)
    ensureToastPool()
    toastToken += 1
    local myToken = toastToken
    -- Dedupe: the same message within 1.5s does not stack again
    -- (kills "Select a target first" style spam).
    local now = tick()
    if HUB.lastToastText == text and now - (HUB.lastToastTime or 0) < 1.5 then
        return
    end
    HUB.lastToastText = text
    HUB.lastToastTime = now
    -- REDESIGN [NOTIFY CONSOLE]: log to history (newest first) -- this is
    -- the notification center feed, always.
    table.insert(HUB.toastHistory, 1, { text = text, colorKey = colorKey, time = now })
    while #HUB.toastHistory > math.clamp(HUB.notifyConfig.historyCap, 4, 64) do
        table.remove(HUB.toastHistory)
    end
    -- REDESIGN: every notification is logged to the center feed (above).
    -- Display mode decides what else happens -- by default nothing floats
    -- over the hub; "legacy" brings back the old in-hub toasts and
    -- "terminal" triggers the bell-rise + CMD drop-down (both toggleable
    -- in the notification center's settings).
    local mode = HUB.notifyConfig.mode or "center"
    if mode == "center" then
        return
    elseif mode == "terminal" then
        if HUB.terminalNotify then HUB.terminalNotify.fire(text, colorKey) end
        return
    end
    -- legacy: floating toasts over the hub, queued through the pool.
    local slot = HUB.toastPool[((toastToken - 1) % #HUB.toastPool) + 1]
    -- Reusing a slot: cancel the previous toast's fade timer so a
    -- newer message can never get faded out (or stuck) by the old one.
    if slot.job then task.cancel(slot.job) slot.job = nil end
    local t = slot.label
    t.Text = text
    t.TextColor3 = (colorKey and COLOR[colorKey]) or COLOR.warn
    t.TextTransparency = 1
    t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    slot.edge.Transparency = 1
    t.Visible = true
    slot.token = myToken
    -- Fade in fast, hold (config duration), fade out.
    TweenService:Create(t, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(t, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(slot.edge, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
    slot.job = task.delay(HUB.notifyConfig.duration, function()
        if slot.token ~= myToken then return end
        TweenService:Create(t, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(t, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(slot.edge, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
        task.delay(0.4, function()
            if slot.token == myToken then t.Visible = false end
        end)
    end)
end
end

local function createDropdown(toggleButton, labelPrefix, options, onSelect)
    toggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if activeOverlay then closeActiveOverlay() return end

        local pos, size = toggleButton.AbsolutePosition, toggleButton.AbsoluteSize
        -- V7: option rows + text grow with the hub size preset.
        local textScale = (currentSizePreset and currentSizePreset.text) or 1
        local optH = math.floor(24 * textScale)
        local listH = optH * #options
        local screenH = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y
        local openUp = (pos.Y + size.Y + listH) > screenH
        local y = openUp and (pos.Y - listH) or (pos.Y + size.Y)

        local panel = newInstance("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, size.X, 0, listH),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, pos.X, 0, y),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, ZIndex = 51, Parent = HUB.overlayLayer,
        })
        round(panel, 6)
        stroke(panel, COLOR.border, 1)
        newInstance("UIListLayout", {Parent = panel, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})

        for _, option in ipairs(options) do
            local optBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, optH),
                BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel,
                Text = option.name, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
                Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = math.max(10, math.floor(12 * textScale)), ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
            })
            bindFont(optBtn, "regular")
            optBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                onSelect(option)
                toggleButton.Text = labelPrefix .. option.name
                closeActiveOverlay()
            end)
        end
        activeOverlay = panel
    end)
end

-- Color picker: opens a compact grid of swatches with a Light/Darken section
-- (shade the pending color) and a Save button that commits it to the color
-- system -- clicking a swatch only marks it, Save commits it.
local pickerPanel = nil
local function closePickers()
    if pickerPanel and pickerPanel.Parent then pickerPanel:Destroy() end
    pickerPanel = nil
end

local function createColorPicker(button, onPick, initial)
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if pickerPanel and pickerPanel.Parent then
            closePickers()
            return
        end
        closePickers()

        -- Grid: capped at 4 visible rows; if the color table ever outgrows that
        -- the swatches scroll instead of pushing the panel off-screen.
        local cols, cellH, maxRows = 4, 24, 4
        local rows = math.ceil(#ColorPalette / cols)
        local visibleRows = math.min(rows, maxRows)
        local pad, gap = 6, 4
        local gridCanvasH = pad * 2 + rows * cellH + (rows - 1) * gap
        local gridH = pad * 2 + visibleRows * cellH + (visibleRows - 1) * gap
        local footH = 64
        local panelH = gridH + footH

        local pending = (initial and initial()) or nil
        -- Light/dark lock: shading is computed relative to the ORIGINAL
        -- (unshaded) color and the factor is clamped to a small band, so a few
        -- presses can never wash the color to white/black.
        local baseColor = pending
        local shadeFactor = 0

        local pos, size = button.AbsolutePosition, button.AbsoluteSize
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vh = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
        local vw = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.X) or 800

        -- Clamp so the table never slides off the right edge on narrow windows.
        local panelW = math.min(236, vw - 8)
        local cellW = (panelW - pad * 2 - gap * (cols - 1)) / cols
        local px = math.max(2, math.min(pos.X + (size.X - panelW) / 2, vw - panelW - 2))
        local openUp = (pos.Y - panelH - 4) < 0
        local y = openUp and math.max(2, pos.Y + size.Y + 4) or math.max(2, pos.Y - panelH - 4)
        y = math.min(y, vh - panelH - 4)

        -- Same stacking setup as the working dropdowns: panel at ZIndex 51 with
        -- every child at ZIndex 51 too, so the swatch grid can never render
        -- behind the panel itself or the window shell.
        local panel = newInstance("Frame", {
            Name = "PickerPanel",
            Size = UDim2.new(0, panelW, 0, panelH),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, px, 0, y),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, ZIndex = 51, Parent = HUB.overlayLayer,
        })
        round(panel, 6)
        stroke(panel, COLOR.border, 1)
        pickerPanel = panel

        local gridHost = newInstance("ScrollingFrame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, gridH),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScrollBarThickness = 4,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, gridCanvasH),
            ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
        })

        local selectedEdge = nil
        local selectedColor = nil
        local selectedCell = nil
        local function selectCell(cell, edge, color)
            if selectedEdge and selectedColor then
                selectedEdge.Color = selectedColor
                selectedEdge.Thickness = 1
            end
            pending = color
            baseColor = color
            shadeFactor = 0
            selectedCell = cell
            selectedEdge = edge
            selectedColor = color
            if cell then cell.BackgroundColor3 = color end
            if edge then
                edge.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                edge.Thickness = 2
            end
        end
        for i, o in ipairs(ColorPalette) do
            local cellColor = o.value
            local cell = newInstance("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, cellW, 0, cellH),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, pad + (i - 1) % cols * (cellW + gap), 0, pad + math.floor((i - 1) / cols) * (cellH + gap)),
                BackgroundColor3 = cellColor,
                Text = o.name,
                TextColor3 = (cellColor.R + cellColor.G + cellColor.B) > 1.8 and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, ZIndex = 51, Parent = gridHost,
            })
            round(cell, 4)
            local edge = newInstance("UIStroke", {Color = cellColor, Thickness = 1, Parent = cell})
            if cellColor == pending then
                selectCell(cell, edge, cellColor)
            end
            cell.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                selectCell(cell, edge, cellColor)
            end)
        end

        -- Light/dark section: a standalone shade group. Each press applies a
        -- FIXED step (0.15) from the original unshaded color and the factor is
        -- clamped to +/-0.45, so a few presses can't wash a slate color to
        -- white or black. Save still commits the shaded value to the theme.
        local lightDarkLabel = newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, gridH + 2),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "LIGHT / DARKEN", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
        })
        bindFont(lightDarkLabel, "bold")

        local SHADE_STEP, SHADE_MAX = 0.15, 0.45
        local function applyShade()
            if not pending then return end
            pending = shadeColor(baseColor, shadeFactor)
            if selectedCell then selectedCell.BackgroundColor3 = pending end
            if selectedEdge then
                selectedEdge.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                selectedEdge.Thickness = 2
            end
            selectedColor = pending
        end

        local lightenBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, gridH + 16),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = "Lighten", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
        })
        round(lightenBtn, 5)
        stroke(lightenBtn, COLOR.border, 1)
        bindFont(lightenBtn, "bold")
        lightenBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            shadeFactor = math.min(shadeFactor + SHADE_STEP, SHADE_MAX)
            applyShade()
        end)

        local darkenBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, gridH + 16),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = "Darken", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
        })
        round(darkenBtn, 5)
        stroke(darkenBtn, COLOR.border, 1)
        bindFont(darkenBtn, "bold")
        darkenBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            shadeFactor = math.max(shadeFactor - SHADE_STEP, -SHADE_MAX)
            applyShade()
        end)

        local saveBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, gridH + 42),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent2, Text = "Save", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
        })
        round(saveBtn, 5)
        bindFont(saveBtn, "bold")
        saveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if pending then
                local ok = pcall(onPick, pending)
                if not ok then showToast("Save failed", "warn") end
            end
            closePickers()
        end)

        local cancelBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, gridH + 42),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Cancel", TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, ZIndex = 51, Parent = panel,
        })
        round(cancelBtn, 5)
        stroke(cancelBtn, COLOR.border, 1)
        bindFont(cancelBtn, "bold")
        cancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            closePickers()
        end)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- TOGGLE SWITCH COMPONENT
-- ============================================================
local function createToggleSwitch(parent, position, style)
    -- Two styles: "check" renders a visible clickable button (highlight on
    -- hover, checkmark when ON, blank when OFF); default is the sliding pill.
    local isCheck = (style == "check")

    local track = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = isCheck and UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 20) or UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20),
        Position = position,
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.switchOff,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = parent,
    })

    local state = false
    local callback = nil
    local knob = nil
    local mark = nil

    local function restColor()
        return state and COLOR.accent or COLOR.switchOff
    end

    if isCheck then
        round(track, 5)
        stroke(track, COLOR.border, 1)
        mark = newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "",
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold,
            TextSize = 15,
            Parent = track,
        })
        bindFont(mark, "bold")
        track.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            track.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(restColor(), -0.16)
        end)
        track.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            track.BackgroundColor3 = restColor()
        end)
    else
        round(track, 10)
        knob = newInstance("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
            Parent = track,
        })
        round(knob, 8)
    end

    table.insert(switchTracks, {track = track, mark = mark, get = function() return state end})

    local function setState(newState, silent)
        state = newState
        if isCheck then
            mark.Text = newState and "\xE2\x9C\x93" or ""
            track.BackgroundColor3 = restColor()
        else
            TweenService:Create(track, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
                BackgroundColor3 = newState and COLOR.accent or COLOR.switchOff
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(knob, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
                Position = newState and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8)
            }):Play()
        end
        if not silent and callback then callback(newState) end
    end

    local function setCallback(fn) callback = fn end

    track.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setState(not state) end)

    return track, setState, function() return state end, setCallback
end

-- ============================================================
-- SECTION BUILDER (labeled toggle row used across every page)
-- id (optional, V4): dotted path like "server.teleport" -- the
-- row registers itself as HUB.rows.server.teleport = { status,
-- set, get, cb }, so wiring code finds rows by name instead of
-- juggling return values. Without an id the legacy 4-return
-- signature is unchanged.
-- ============================================================
local function createSwitchRow(parent, y, headerText, keyHint, id, style)
    local header = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, y),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = headerText .. (keyHint and ("  (" .. keyHint .. ")") or ""),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = parent,
    })
    bindFont(header, "bold")

    local status = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, y),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Off", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.off, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = parent,
    })
    bindFont(status, "bold")

    local togglePos = (style == "check") and UDim2.new(1, -27, 0, y - 2) or UDim2.new(1, -45, 0, y - 2)
    local track, setSwitch, getSwitch, setSwitchCallback = createToggleSwitch(parent, togglePos, style)
    if id then
        local parts = {}
        for part in string.gmatch(id, "[^%.]+") do parts[#parts + 1] = part end
        local node = HUB.rows
        for i = 1, #parts - 1 do
            node[parts[i]] = node[parts[i]] or {}
            node = node[parts[i]]
        end
        node[parts[#parts]] = { status = status, set = setSwitch, get = getSwitch, cb = setSwitchCallback }
    end
    return status, setSwitch, getSwitch, setSwitchCallback
end

-- ============================================================
-- SERVER PAGE CURSOR (V6): the scrollable Server sub-tab appends
-- every row below the last one through serverRow()/serverSection(),
-- so no absolute-y math can collide again. The final builder sets
-- the canvas height.
-- ============================================================
HUB.serverY = 6
local function serverSection(text)
    HUB.serverY = HUB.serverY + 10
    local tick = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, HUB.serverY + 3),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent2, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = HUB.views.server,
    })
    round(tick, 2)
    HUB.collapsibleHeader(HUB.views.server, text, HUB.serverY)
    HUB.serverY = HUB.serverY + 14 + 10
end
local function serverRow(text, keyHint, id)
    local status, set, get, cb = createSwitchRow(HUB.views.server, HUB.serverY, text, keyHint, id)
    HUB.serverY = HUB.serverY + 30
    return status, set, get, cb
end

    currentBorderPreset = preset

end
local function applyCornerStyle(preset)
    TweenService:Create(frameCorner, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, preset.radius)}):Play()
end

-- REDESIGN QoL 3: double-click the title bar to recenter the window on
-- the screen (handy after dragging it somewhere).
;(function()
    local lastTitleDbl = 0
    HUB.title.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastTitleDbl < 0.35 then
            lastTitleDbl = 0
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vp = (cam and cam.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
            local fsz = HUB.frame.AbsoluteSize
            HUB.frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.floor((vp.X - fsz.X) / 2), 0, math.floor((vp.Y - fsz.Y) / 2))
            showToast("Window centered", "textDim")
        else
            lastTitleDbl = now
        end
    end)
end)()

-- REDESIGN: side flyout menu (replaces the old bottom quick-nav bar).
-- A small tab sticks out of the window's right edge; hovering it opens a
-- FIXED-SIZE panel to the RIGHT of the tab, outside the window (so it
-- never covers hub content). Shared hover behavior via wireExternalHover.
;(function()
    local expandedW, expandedH = 120, 6 * 22 + 5 * 3 + 2 * 6

    local tab = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, 3, 0.5, -12),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.titleBar, Text = "≡",
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12,
Parent = HUB.frame, ZIndex = 45,
    })
    round(tab, 8)
    stroke(tab, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(tab, "bold")

    local panel = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, expandedW, 0, expandedH),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 28, 0.5, -expandedH / 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.titleBar, Visible = false, Parent = HUB.frame, ZIndex = 47,
    })
    round(panel, 8)
    stroke(panel, COLOR.border, 1)
    newInstance("UIListLayout", { Parent = panel, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 3) })
    -- UIPadding centers the buttons inside the panel (equal gutters on
    -- both sides) instead of the old left-aligned look.
    newInstance("UIPadding", {
        Parent = panel, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6),
    })

    local closeFlyout
    local function flyoutItem(text, fn)
        local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
            -- [FIX] brighter fill (playerBtn +22% toward white) so the rows
            -- can never melt into the dark panel; ZIndex 49 keeps them above
            -- every HUB.frame sibling (terminal 48, notify 45) while staying
            -- below the overlayLayer pickers (50).
            BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(COLOR.playerBtn, 0.22), Text = text, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = panel,
            ZIndex = 49,
        })
        round(b, 6)
        stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
        bindFont(b, "bold")
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            fn()
            if closeFlyout then closeFlyout() end
        end)
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(COLOR.playerBtn, 0.06) end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(COLOR.playerBtn, 0.22) end)
        return b
    end
    flyoutItem("TP", function() HUB.showPage("main") HUB.showTpSubTab("TP") end)
    flyoutItem("Ply Handler", function() HUB.showPage("main") HUB.showTpSubTab("Ply Handler") end)
    flyoutItem("Chase", function() HUB.showPage("chase") end)
    flyoutItem("Console+", function() HUB.showPage("extras") end)
    flyoutItem("Settings", function() HUB.showPage("settings") end)
    flyoutItem("Stop All", function() if HUB.stopEverything then HUB.stopEverything() end end)

    -- Hover to open, leave to close. Panel pops to the RIGHT of the ≡
    -- button, outside the window, so it never covers hub content.
    closeFlyout = wireExternalHover(tab, panel, "flyout",
        function() HUB.externalUI.open("flyout") end, COLOR.titleBar)
end)()

-- ============================================================
-- REDESIGN [NOTIFY CENTER]: side notification center on the RIGHT edge,
-- directly below the quick (≡) button (same style as the flyout).
-- Hovering the bell opens the panel to the RIGHT of it, outside the
-- window (hover-based, shared wireExternalHover behavior). The panel has
-- two views built at load -- HISTORY (all notifications and customization
-- updates are logged here) and SETTINGS (gear: history cap, floating
-- toasts on/off, back). Only one external UI can be open at a time
-- (shared with the ≡ flyout via HUB.externalUI).
-- ============================================================
;(function()
    local expandedW, expandedH = 230, 220

    -- Right edge, one tab-height below the flyout (≡) at (1,3,0.5,-12).
    local tab = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, 3, 0.5, -40),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.titleBar, Text = "🔔",
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12,
        Parent = HUB.frame, ZIndex = 45,
    })
    round(tab, 8)
    stroke(tab, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(tab, "bold")

    -- Pops to the RIGHT of the bell tab, outside the window, so it never
    -- covers the hub content (clicking through to players/toggles was the
    -- old left-side behavior). Top-aligned with the tab.
    local panel = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, expandedW, 0, expandedH),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 28, 0.5, -52),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.titleBar, Visible = false, Parent = HUB.frame, ZIndex = 45,
    })
    round(panel, 8)
    stroke(panel, COLOR.border, 1)

    -- Header: SETTINGS ONLY navigates to the settings view. APPLY (a
    -- download/update-styled link, distinct green) commits staged settings.
    -- CLEAR wipes history. Flat text links -- no boxy icon buttons.
    local commitSettings, showView, renderSettings
    newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "NOTIF",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = panel, ZIndex = 46,
    })
    local applyBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -122, 0, 7),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "↓ APPLY", TextColor3 = COLOR.on,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 8, Parent = panel, ZIndex = 46,
    })
    bindFont(applyBtn, "bold")
    applyBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() applyBtn.TextColor3 = COLOR.text end)
    applyBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() applyBtn.TextColor3 = COLOR.on end)
    applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() commitSettings() end)
    local settingsBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, 7),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "SETTINGS", TextColor3 = COLOR.accent,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 8, Parent = panel, ZIndex = 46,
    })
    bindFont(settingsBtn, "bold")
    settingsBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() settingsBtn.TextColor3 = COLOR.text end)
    settingsBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() settingsBtn.TextColor3 = COLOR.accent end)
    settingsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showView("settings") end)
    local clearBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 7),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "CLEAR", TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 8, Parent = panel, ZIndex = 46,
    })
    bindFont(clearBtn, "bold")
    clearBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() clearBtn.TextColor3 = COLOR.text end)
    clearBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() clearBtn.TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim end)

    local histList = newInstance("ScrollingFrame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -36), Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 30),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScrollBarThickness = 3,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 5, 0), Parent = panel,
    })
    newInstance("UIListLayout", {Parent = histList, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)})

    -- Empty state: the panel always shows something, so it can never
    -- look like a blank box when there is no toast history yet.
    local emptyLabel = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 34),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "No notifications yet",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
        Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 10, Parent = panel, ZIndex = 46,
    })
    bindFont(emptyLabel, "regular")

    local function renderHistory()
        pcall(function()
            for _, child in ipairs(histList:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
            end
            local entries = HUB.toastHistory or {}
            emptyLabel.Visible = #entries == 0
            histList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(#entries * 24 - 2, 0))
            for _, e in ipairs(entries) do
                local row = newInstance("TextButton", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
                    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn,
                    Text = tostring(e.text), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextColor3 = (e.colorKey and COLOR[e.colorKey]) or COLOR.warn,
                    Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 10, Parent = histList,
                })
                round(row, 4)
                bindFont(row, "regular")
                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    pcall(function() setclipboard(e.text) end)
                    showToast("Copied: " .. e.text, "textDim")
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    clearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        HUB.toastHistory = {}
        renderHistory()
        showToast("History cleared", "textDim")
    end)

    -- ---- SETTINGS view (built once at load -- nothing can go blank) ----
    -- Staged model: picking an option only stages into `pending`; nothing
    -- takes effect until APPLY (header link OR the settings-view APPLY
    -- button) commits it. SETTINGS only navigates.
    local pending = {
        mode = HUB.notifyConfig.mode,
        historyCap = HUB.notifyConfig.historyCap,
        duration = HUB.notifyConfig.duration,
        maxSlots = HUB.notifyConfig.maxSlots,
        anchor = HUB.notifyConfig.anchor,
    }
    local settingsView = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -36), Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 30),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, Parent = panel,
    })
    local optionRow
    optionRow = function(y, label, options, current, onPick)
        newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, y),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = label,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = settingsView, ZIndex = 46,
        })
        local n = #options
        local bw = (230 - 14 - (n - 1) * 4) / n
        for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
            local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, bw, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, 7 + (i - 1) * (bw + 4), 0, y + 16),
                BackgroundColor3 = opt.value == current() and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn,
                Text = opt.name, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
                Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 9, Parent = settingsView, ZIndex = 46,
            })
            round(b, 4)
            stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
            bindFont(b, "bold")
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                onPick(opt.value)
                renderSettings()
            end)
        end
        return y + 40
    end
    renderSettings = function()
        pcall(function()
            for _, child in ipairs(settingsView:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
            end
            local y = 8
            y = optionRow(y, "HISTORY CAP", {
                {name = "8", value = 8}, {name = "16", value = 16},
                {name = "32", value = 32}, {name = "64", value = 64},
            }, function() return pending.historyCap end, function(v) pending.historyCap = v end)
            y = optionRow(y, "DISPLAY MODE", {
                {name = "CENTER", value = "center"},
                {name = "LEGACY", value = "legacy"},
                {name = "TERMINAL", value = "terminal"},
            }, function() return pending.mode end, function(v) pending.mode = v end)
            local resetBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y + 4),
                BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = "RESET TO DEFAULTS", TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
                Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = settingsView, ZIndex = 46,
            })
            round(resetBtn, 6)
            stroke(resetBtn, COLOR.border, 1)
            bindFont(resetBtn, "bold")
            resetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                pending.mode = "center"
                pending.historyCap = 12
                pending.duration = 7.5
                pending.maxSlots = 3
                pending.anchor = "bottom-left"
                renderSettings()
                showToast("Defaults staged - press APPLY", "textDim")
            end)
            local applyBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y + 30),
                BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.on, Text = "↓ APPLY", TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
                Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = settingsView, ZIndex = 46,
            })
            round(applyBtn, 6)
            bindFont(applyBtn, "bold")
            applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() commitSettings() end)
            local backBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y + 56),
                BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent, Text = "← BACK", TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
                Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = settingsView, ZIndex = 46,
            })
            round(backBtn, 6)
            bindFont(backBtn, "bold")
            backBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showView("history") end)
        end)
    end

    showView = function(name)
        if name == "settings" then
            renderSettings()
            settingsView.Visible = true
            histList.Visible = false
            emptyLabel.Visible = false
        else
            renderHistory()
            settingsView.Visible = false
            histList.Visible = true
        end
    end

    -- Commit staged settings: writes pending into the live config, then
    -- applies side effects (drop floating toasts when leaving legacy,
    -- clear any terminal strip, trim history to the new cap).
    commitSettings = function()
        local cfg = HUB.notifyConfig
        local modeChanged = cfg.mode ~= pending.mode
        cfg.mode = pending.mode
        cfg.historyCap = pending.historyCap
        cfg.duration = pending.duration
        cfg.maxSlots = pending.maxSlots
        cfg.anchor = pending.anchor
        if modeChanged and pending.mode ~= "legacy" then
            for _, slot in ipairs(HUB.toastPool) do
                if slot.job then task.cancel(slot.job) slot.job = nil end
                if slot.label then slot.label.Visible = false end
            end
        end
        if HUB.terminalNotify then HUB.terminalNotify.clear() end
        local cap = HUB.toastHistory and #HUB.toastHistory or 0
        if cap > cfg.historyCap then
            local trimmed = {}
            for i = math.max(1, cap - cfg.historyCap + 1), cap do
                trimmed[#trimmed + 1] = HUB.toastHistory[i]
            end
            HUB.toastHistory = trimmed
        end
        showToast("Notification settings applied", "textDim")
        showView("history")
    end

    -- Hover to open, leave to close (same behavior as the ≡ flyout). The
    -- panel opens to the RIGHT of the bell, outside the window. Only one
    -- external UI open at a time (shared with the ≡ flyout). Opening the
    -- center also clears any transient terminal notification so the two
    -- never stack on top of each other.
    wireExternalHover(tab, panel, "notify", function()
        HUB.externalUI.open("notify")
        if HUB.terminalNotify then HUB.terminalNotify.clear() end
        showView("history")
    end, COLOR.titleBar)

    -- ---- TERMINAL mode (DISPLAY MODE = TERMINAL in the center settings) ----
    -- When a notification fires, the BELL RISES (like a browser tab popping
    -- up) and holds, and a single-line notification strip attaches directly
    -- to its underside -- the bell is the tab, the strip is its content --
    -- so the two read as ONE connected element, not separate UIs. Green
    -- monospace on near-black, fixed colors + Code font so it keeps its
    -- escape-terminal identity no matter what the hub looks like.
    local termGreen = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 96)
    local termYellow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70)
    local termRed = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
    local termBg = Color3.fromRGB(9, 12, 10)
    local termBorder = Color3.fromRGB(34, 96, 58)
    local termFont = Enum.Font.Code
    local bellBasePos = UDim2.new(1, 3, 0.5, -40)
    local bellUpPos = UDim2.new(1, 3, 0.5, -50)

    -- Content strip: left edge flush with the bell, zero gap under its
    -- underside, so it hangs off the risen bell like a tab's body.
    local termBar = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 22),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 3, 0.5, -26),
        BackgroundColor3 = termBg, Visible = false, Parent = HUB.frame, ZIndex = 48,
    })
    round(termBar, 6)
    stroke(termBar, termBorder, 1)
    -- Left accent strip: subtle green edge so the strip reads as a live
    -- terminal line even when the message is short.
    local termAccent = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -4), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = termGreen, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = termBar,
    })
    round(termAccent, 1.5)
    local termText = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 2),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextColor3 = termGreen, Font = termFont, TextSize = 11,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = termBar,
    })
    newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 7),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "●",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextColor3 = termRed,
        Font = termFont, TextSize = 10, Parent = termBar,
    })

    local function termColorFor(k)
        if k == "warn" then return termYellow end
        if k == "red" or k == "error" then return termRed end
        return termGreen
    end
    local termJob = nil
    -- Blinking block cursor after the message (terminal look). Runs only
    -- while the strip is visible; stopped on hide.
    local termBlinkJob = nil
    local termPlain = ""
    local function termStartBlink()
        if termBlinkJob then task.cancel(termBlinkJob) end
        termBlinkJob = task.spawn(function()
            while termBar.Visible do
                termText.Text = termPlain .. "▊"
                task.wait(0.5)
                termText.Text = termPlain
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function termHide()
        if termJob then task.cancel(termJob) termJob = nil end
        if termBlinkJob then task.cancel(termBlinkJob) termBlinkJob = nil end
        termBar.Visible = false
        termText.Text = ""
        TweenService:Create(tab, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = bellBasePos }):Play()
    end
    local function termFire(text, colorKey)
        termPlain = "> " .. tostring(text)
        termText.TextColor3 = termColorFor(colorKey)
        termBar.Visible = true
        termStartBlink()
        TweenService:Create(tab, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = bellUpPos }):Play()
        if termJob then task.cancel(termJob) end
        termJob = task.delay(4, function() termJob = nil termHide() end)
    end
    HUB.terminalNotify = { fire = termFire, clear = termHide }
end)()

-- Drag
do
    local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos
    HUB.title.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging, dragStart, startPos = true, input.Position, HUB.frame.Position
            closeActiveOverlay()
        end
    end)
    HUB.title.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then dragInput = input end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input == dragInput and dragging then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            HUB.frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
        end
    end)
    HUB.title.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
end

-- TAB BAR

HUB.tabBar = newInstance("Frame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 33),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = HUB.frame,
})

-- V6: per-tab state (active refresh fn + page id) kept in a plain table;
-- Roblox instances reject custom members, so nothing hangs off the buttons.
local tabStates = {}
-- [V6 FIX] Forward declaration: Luau locals are only in scope AFTER their
-- declaration. The tab click closures below reference showPage, which is
-- defined much later (Part E) -- without this forward decl the closures
-- would bind the GLOBAL showPage (nil), killing every tab switch and any
-- button that routes through showPage at runtime.
local showPage

local function makeTab(text, index, pageId)
    local count = 4
    local btn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1/count, -3, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new((index - 1)/count, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = text, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = HUB.tabBar,
    })
    round(btn, 6)
    bindFont(btn, "bold")
    -- V6 design: hover brightens the tab; the active tab gets an
    -- accent2 underline strip instead of a full background change.
    local underline = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 2), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -3),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent2,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, Parent = btn,
    })
    bindThemed(underline, "BackgroundColor3", "accent2")
    local function refreshTabState()
        local active = HUB.currentPage == pageId
        underline.Visible = active
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = active and COLOR.accent2 or COLOR.panel
        btn.TextColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or COLOR.text
    end
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if HUB.currentPage ~= pageId then btn.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(COLOR.panel, -0.12) end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = (HUB.currentPage == pageId) and COLOR.accent2 or COLOR.panel
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        showPage(pageId)
        refreshTabState()
    end)
    -- [V6 FIX] Instances reject custom fields ("X is not a valid member
    -- of TextButton"), so tab bookkeeping lives in a script-side table
    -- keyed by the button instead of on the button itself.
    tabStates[btn] = { refresh = refreshTabState, pageId = pageId }
    return btn
end

HUB.tabs.main = makeTab("TP+", 1, "main")
HUB.tabs.chase = makeTab("Chase", 2, "chase")
HUB.tabs.extras = makeTab("Console+", 3, "extras")
HUB.tabs.settings = makeTab("Settings", 4, "settings")

    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = MainPage,
})
local PlyHandlerView = newInstance("Frame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, Parent = MainPage,
})
HUB.tpSubViews[1], HUB.tpSubViews[2] = MainTpView, PlyHandlerView

local function showTpSubTab(id)
    HUB.tpSubTab = id
    _G.schizhubLastTpSub = id
    for i, name in ipairs(tpSubOrder) do
        local active = (name == id)
        HUB.tpSubButtons[i].BackgroundColor3 = active and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
        HUB.tpSubViews[i].Visible = active
    end
end
HUB.showTpSubTab = showTpSubTab

HUB.tpSubButtons = {}
for i, name in ipairs(tpSubOrder) do
    local tabName = name
    local btn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1/#tpSubOrder, -5, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) / #tpSubOrder, (i == 1) and 5 or 2, 0.5, -8),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = name, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = HUB.tpSubBar,
    })
    round(btn, 4)
    bindFont(btn, "bold")
    bindThemed(btn, "BackgroundColor3", function()
        return (HUB.tpSubTab == tabName) and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
    end)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if HUB.tpSubTab ~= tabName then btn.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(COLOR.playerBtn, -0.12) end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = (HUB.tpSubTab == tabName) and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showTpSubTab(tabName) end)
    HUB.tpSubButtons[i] = btn
end

-- REDESIGN QoL 5: footer hint on the TP view -- first-time users know

-- SETTINGS PAGE

-- Scrolling settings: keeps every customization reachable as new sections
-- get added, without having to redesign the page each time.
local SettingsPage = newInstance("ScrollingFrame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -63), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 63),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ScrollBarThickness = 4,
