-- Vertical cursor layout: each section appends itself below the previous one,
-- so the page reflows automatically and new sections slot in without any
-- hand-tuned pixel math. Headers ride the hub-size text scaling like every
-- other label (see applyTextScale), so no separate header-size registry.
-- V6: headers get an accent tick + left alignment (design overhaul).
local settingsY = 0
local function settingsHeader(text)
    settingsY = settingsY + 10
    local tick = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, settingsY + 3),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent2, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = SettingsPage,
    })
    round(tick, 2)
    HUB.collapsibleHeader(SettingsPage, text, settingsY)
    settingsY = settingsY + 14 + 6
end

-- ============================================================
-- UI PRESETS (V6: whole-look bookmarks -- theme / size / font /
-- border / edging -- switchable from Settings. No JSON, no files:
-- slots live in memory, _G carries them across same-session
-- re-injects, and Copy emits a snippet to paste into
-- IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS at the top of this script for permanence.
-- One script, one hub.)
-- ============================================================
local UiPresetSlots = {}
for i, imp in ipairs(IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS) do
    if type(imp) == "table" then UiPresetSlots[i] = imp end
end

local function uiPresetCapture()
    return {
        name = currentPreset.name,
        colors = {
            accent = COLOR.accent, accent2 = COLOR.accent2, accent3 = COLOR.accent3,
            bg = COLOR.bg, text = COLOR.text,
        },
        size = currentSizePreset.name,
        text = HUB.currentTextPreset and HUB.currentTextPreset.name or "Normal",
        font = currentFontPreset.name,
        border = currentBorderPreset.name,
        edging = currentEdgingFilter and currentEdgingFilter.name or "Default",
    }
end

local function uiPresetApply(slot, silent)
    if not slot then return end
    if slot.colors and slot.colors.accent then
        applyTheme({
            name = slot.name or "Custom",
            accent = slot.colors.accent,
            accent2 = slot.colors.accent2 or COLOR.accent2,
            accent3 = slot.colors.accent3 or COLOR.accent3,
            bg = slot.colors.bg,
            text = slot.colors.text,
        })
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(SIZE_PRESETS) do
        if p.name == slot.size then applySize(p) break end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(HUB.TEXT_SCALE_PRESETS) do
        if p.name == slot.text then applyTextPreset(p) break end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(FONT_PRESETS) do
        if p.name == slot.font then applyFont(p) break end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(BORDER_EDGING_PRESETS) do
        if p.name == slot.border then applyBorderStyle(p) applyCornerStyle(p) break end
    end
    if HUB.applyEdgingFilterByName then HUB.applyEdgingFilterByName(slot.edging) end
    if not silent then showToast("UI preset: " .. (slot.name or "?"), "on") end
end

local function uiPresetSave(i)
    local cap = uiPresetCapture()
    cap.name = currentPreset.name .. " " .. tostring(i)
    UiPresetSlots[i] = cap
    _G.schizhubUiPresets = UiPresetSlots
    _G.schizhubLastUiSlot = i
    return cap
end

-- Emits the Lua snippet for the slot; paste it into IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS
-- at the top of this script to make the look permanent.
local function uiPresetCopy(i)
    local slot = UiPresetSlots[i]
    if not slot then return false end
    local c = slot.colors or {}
    local lines = {
        "IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS[" .. tostring(i) .. "] = {",
        "    name = " .. string.format("%q", slot.name or "Custom") .. ",",
        "    colors = {",
        "        accent = Color3.fromRGB(" .. PaletteRgb(c.accent or COLOR.accent) .. "),",
        "        accent2 = Color3.fromRGB(" .. PaletteRgb(c.accent2 or COLOR.accent2) .. "),",
        "        accent3 = Color3.fromRGB(" .. PaletteRgb(c.accent3 or COLOR.accent3) .. "),",
        "        bg = Color3.fromRGB(" .. PaletteRgb(c.bg or COLOR.bg) .. "),",
        "        text = Color3.fromRGB(" .. PaletteRgb(c.text or COLOR.text) .. "),",
        "    },",
        "    size = " .. string.format("%q", slot.size or "Medium") .. ",",
        "    font = " .. string.format("%q", slot.font or "Gotham") .. ",",
        "    border = " .. string.format("%q", slot.border or "Default") .. ",",
        "    edging = " .. string.format("%q", slot.edging or "Default") .. ",",
        "},",
    }
    local ok = pcall(setclipboard, table.concat(lines, "\n"))
    return ok
end

-- The whole page build lives in its own function so the main chunk stays under
-- Luau's 200-local-register cap as the settings sections keep growing.
local function buildSettingsPage()
-- Edging color filters: derives a border color from the current mode and
-- pushes it into the theme system so every themed edge updates at once.
-- Background is preselected (looked up by mode, not array index, since the
-- Background button sits on its own row above the centered trio).
local edgingFilterBtns = {}
-- [FIX 1] lighten/darken derive from the live theme border (COLOR.border)
-- instead of the hardcoded defaultBorder, and darken uses a 0.47 multiplier.
local function edgingFilterColor(filter)
    if filter.mode == "accent" then return COLOR.accent end
    if filter.mode == "accent2" then return COLOR.accent2 end
    if filter.mode == "bg" then return COLOR.bg end
    if filter.mode == "lighten" then
        local c = COLOR.border
        local l = 0.35
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + (255 - c.R * 255) * l),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + (255 - c.G * 255) * l),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + (255 - c.B * 255) * l)
        )
    end
    if filter.mode == "darken" then
        local c = COLOR.border
        return Color3.fromRGB(math.floor(c.R * 255 * 0.47), math.floor(c.G * 255 * 0.47), math.floor(c.B * 255 * 0.47))
    end
    return defaultBorder
end
local function updateEdgingFilterLabels()
    for _, e in ipairs(edgingFilterBtns) do
        local active = (e.filter == currentEdgingFilter)
        e.btn.BackgroundColor3 = active and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
        e.btn.TextColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30) or COLOR.text
    end
end
local function applyEdgingFilter(filter)
    currentEdgingFilter = filter
    COLOR.border = edgingFilterColor(filter)
    refreshTheme()
    updateEdgingFilterLabels()
end

settingsHeader("HUB SIZE")
for i, preset in ipairs(SIZE_PRESETS) do
    local btn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1/#SIZE_PRESETS, -3, 0, 26),

        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = preset.name, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 11, Parent = SettingsPage,
    })
    round(btn, 6)
    stroke(btn, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(btn, "regular")
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        applySize(preset)
        showToast("Size: " .. preset.name, "textDim")
    end)
end
settingsY = settingsY + 26 + 6

-- Name-based edging filter apply, shared with the UI PRESETS system.
HUB.applyEdgingFilterByName = function(name)
    for _, f in ipairs(EDGING_FILTERS) do
        if f.name == name then
            applyEdgingFilter(f)
            return
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- UI PRESETS (bookmark the current look into a slot; LOAD switches
-- to it; COPY puts a snippet on the clipboard to paste into
-- IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS at the top of the script for permanence)
-- ============================================================
settingsHeader("UI PRESETS")
local uiSelectedSlot = 1
local uiSlotBtns = {}
local function uiRenderSlots()
    for i, b in ipairs(uiSlotBtns) do
        local slot = UiPresetSlots[i]
        b.Text = slot and slot.name or tostring(i)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = (i == uiSelectedSlot) and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
        b.TextColor3 = (i == uiSelectedSlot) and Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30) or COLOR.text
    end
end
for i = 1, 5 do
    local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.2, -4, 0, 24),
        Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.2, (i == 1) and 5 or 2, 0, settingsY),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = tostring(i), TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = SettingsPage,
    })
    round(b, 6)
    stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(b, "bold")
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        uiSelectedSlot = i
        uiRenderSlots()
    end)
    uiSlotBtns[i] = b
end
settingsY = settingsY + 24 + 4

local function uiPresetActionBtn(col, text, cb)
    local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1/3, -4, 0, 24),
        Position = UDim2.new(col / 3, col == 0 and 5 or 2, 0, settingsY),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = text, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = SettingsPage,
    })
    round(b, 6)
    stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(b, "bold")
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
    return b
end
uiPresetActionBtn(0, "SAVE", function()
    uiPresetSave(uiSelectedSlot)
    uiRenderSlots()
    showToast("Saved to slot " .. uiSelectedSlot, "on")
end)
uiPresetActionBtn(1, "LOAD", function()
    if not UiPresetSlots[uiSelectedSlot] then
        showToast("Slot " .. uiSelectedSlot .. " is empty", "warn")
        return
    end
    uiPresetApply(UiPresetSlots[uiSelectedSlot], false)
    uiRenderSlots()
end)
uiPresetActionBtn(2, "COPY", function()
    if not UiPresetSlots[uiSelectedSlot] then
        showToast("Slot " .. uiSelectedSlot .. " is empty", "warn")
        return
    end
    if uiPresetCopy(uiSelectedSlot) then
        showToast("Copied -- paste into IMPORTED_UI_PRESETS", "on")
    else
        showToast("Clipboard unavailable on this executor", "warn")
    end
end)
settingsY = settingsY + 24 + 6
uiRenderSlots()

end
buildSettingsPage()

-- ============================================================
-- EXTRAS PAGE (console-first with ESP / Server sub-tabs)
-- ============================================================
-- The extras tab is console-first: the CNSL view shows the command bar with
-- the command help list beneath it. A sub-tab bar below the main tabs switches
-- between the sections. The window auto-grows when this tab is shown
-- (see showPage / setMinimized).
HUB.currentPage = "main"
local EXTRAS_CONTENT_H = 475 -- [FIX 5] extras window height budget kept for the TP sub-tab (reserved for a future feature)
-- Auto-adjust: every CNSL+ sub-tab EXCEPT TP sizes the window to its own
-- content height, so the hub fits the active view instead of always being
-- full-height. TP keeps the fixed budget above since a feature is planned there.
-- [FIX 4] CNSL bumped 180 -> 240 so the console content never clips at small hub sizes.
-- REDESIGN [MOVE]: ESP shrunk to 360 (AIM/OVERLAYS left for Server);
-- Server grew to ~700 (now hosts AIM + OVERLAYS).
local SUBTAB_CONTENT_H = { ESP = 360, SPD = 150, CNSL = 240, Server = 700 }
local function getViewportHeight()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    return (cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
end
local function extrasWindowHeight(sub)
    if sub == "TP" then
        return math.min(63 + EXTRAS_CONTENT_H + 2, getViewportHeight() - 40)
    end
    -- REDESIGN [SMART REFLOW]: prefer the live visible content bottom so
    -- collapsed sections keep the window snug; fall back to the static
    -- per-sub content budget.
    local view = (sub == "ESP" and HUB.views.esp) or (sub == "SPD" and HUB.views.spd)
        or (sub == "CNSL" and HUB.views.console) or (sub == "TP" and HUB.views.tp)
        or HUB.views.server
    local live = view and HUB.contentBottom[view]
    local h = live or (SUBTAB_CONTENT_H[sub] or 180)
    return math.min(83 + h + 8, getViewportHeight() - 40)
end
HUB.extrasWindowHeight = extrasWindowHeight

    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -83), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 83),

})

-- ============================================================
-- SUB-TAB VIEW REGISTRY + SWITCHING
-- ============================================================
-- Views are registered here (HUB.views.esp / spd / console / tp /
-- server, plus the ordered subTabViews mirror). The section below
-- renders the sub-tab bar and swaps the visible view; the active
-- highlight is live-bound so theme refreshes (function keys) never
-- leave a stale color.
HUB.views.esp = EspView
HUB.views.spd = SpdView
HUB.views.console = ConsoleView
HUB.views.tp = TpView
HUB.views.server = ServerView
HUB.subTabViews[1], HUB.subTabViews[2], HUB.subTabViews[3], HUB.subTabViews[4], HUB.subTabViews[5] = EspView, SpdView, ConsoleView, TpView, ServerView

local function showExtrasSubTab(id)
    HUB.extrasSubTab = id
    _G.schizhubLastExtrasSub = id
    for i, name in ipairs(subTabOrder) do
        local active = (name == id)
        HUB.subTabButtons[i].BackgroundColor3 = active and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
        HUB.subTabViews[i].Visible = active
    end
    -- Auto-adjust: the window re-heights itself to the active sub-tab while
    -- the extras page is open (TP excluded -- it keeps its reserved budget).
    -- Uses the shared fit so the VIEW is also sized to the window -- the
    -- Server tab content scrolls inside the window instead of going off-view.
    if HUB.currentPage == "extras" then
        if id == "TP" then
            HUB.frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, HUB.winW, 0, extrasWindowHeight("TP"))
        else
            local view = (id == "ESP" and HUB.views.esp) or (id == "SPD" and HUB.views.spd)
                or (id == "CNSL" and HUB.views.console) or HUB.views.server
            HUB.fitExtrasWindow(view)
        end
    end
end

for i, name in ipairs(subTabOrder) do
    local tabName = name
    local btn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1/#subTabOrder, -5, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) / #subTabOrder, (i == 1) and 5 or 2, 0.5, -8),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = name, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = HUB.subTabBar,
    })
    round(btn, 4)
    bindFont(btn, "bold")
    bindThemed(btn, "BackgroundColor3", function()
        return (HUB.extrasSubTab == tabName) and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
    end)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if HUB.extrasSubTab ~= tabName then btn.BackgroundColor3 = shadeColor(COLOR.playerBtn, -0.12) end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = (HUB.extrasSubTab == tabName) and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showExtrasSubTab(tabName) end)
    HUB.subTabButtons[i] = btn
end
showExtrasSubTab("CNSL")
-- PART F1. TP SUB-TAB (save / load / random position,
-- with a per-instance Teleport Bank of saved locations)
-- ============================================================

local function getMyRoot()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    return char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
end

local tpBank = {}
local tpSelected = nil

    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),

})
local TpPopupPanel = newInstance("Frame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(0, 190, 0, 96), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -95, 0.5, -48),

    Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),

})
local TpPopupMsg = newInstance("TextLabel", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 26),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,

})
local TpPopupSub = newInstance("TextLabel", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,

    Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 64),

    TpPopup.Visible = false
end)
local tpPopupToken = 0
local function tpPopupError(msg, sub)
    TpPopupMsg.Text = msg
    TpPopupSub.Text = sub or ""
    TpPopup.Visible = true
    tpPopupToken += 1
    local myToken = tpPopupToken
    task.delay(2.5, function()
        if myToken == tpPopupToken then TpPopup.Visible = false end
    end)
end

local TpPosLabel = select(2, HUB.collapsibleHeader(TpView, "POSITION", 4))
bindFont(TpPosLabel, "bold")

local function tpBtn(y, text, cb)
    local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, y),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = text, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = TpView,
    })
    round(b, 6)
    stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(b, "bold")
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
    return b
end

HUB.collapsibleHeader(TpView, "TELEPORT BANK", 116)

local TpBankFrame = newInstance("ScrollingFrame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 82), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 132),
    CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), ScrollBarThickness = 3,
    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Parent = TpView,
})
round(TpBankFrame, 6)
stroke(TpBankFrame, COLOR.border, 1)
newInstance("UIListLayout", {Parent = TpBankFrame, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)})

local function tpRenderBank()
    for _, child in ipairs(TpBankFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    TpBankFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(#tpBank * 26 - 2, 0))
    for i, pos in ipairs(tpBank) do
        local row = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
            BackgroundColor3 = (i == tpSelected) and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn,
            Text = ("%d \xC2\xB7 %.0f, %.0f, %.0f"):format(i, pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z),
            TextColor3 = COLOR.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 10, Parent = TpBankFrame,
        })
        round(row, 4)
        bindFont(row, "regular")
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            tpSelected = i
            tpRenderBank()
            local root = getMyRoot()
            if root then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
                showToast("Teleported to save " .. i, "on")
            else
                showToast("No character", "warn")
            end
        end)
    end
end

tpBtn(22, "💾 Save Position", function()
    local root = getMyRoot()
    if not root then showToast("No character", "warn") return end
    local pos = root.Position
    for j, saved in ipairs(tpBank) do
        if (saved - pos).Magnitude < 15 then
            tpPopupError("Too close to save " .. j, "(within 15 studs)")
            return
        end
    end
    table.insert(tpBank, pos)
    tpSelected = #tpBank
    tpRenderBank()
    showToast("Position saved", "on")
end)

tpBtn(54, "📂 Load Position", function()
    local root = getMyRoot()
    if not root then showToast("No character", "warn") return end
    local pos = tpBank[tpSelected]
    if not pos then
        showToast("Save a position first", "warn")
        return
    end
    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
    showToast("Loaded save " .. tpSelected, "on")
end)

tpBtn(86, "🎲 Teleport Random", function()
    local root = getMyRoot()
    if not root then showToast("No character", "warn") return end
    local targets = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then table.insert(targets, hrp) end
        end
    end
    if #targets > 0 then
        local t = targets[math.random(1, #targets)]
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(t.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
    else
        showToast("No other players", "warn")
    end
end)
-- REDESIGN [SMART REFLOW]: extras TP view content bottom (POSITION +
-- TELEPORT BANK sections).
HUB.contentBottom[TpView] = 214

-- ============================================================
-- PART F2. SPD SUB-TAB (KACHOW speed: slider + same presets as chase/follow)
-- Exposes its mode API for the :kachow command and the easter egg.
-- ============================================================
local kachowApi = {}
do
    local SpdView = HUB.views.spd
    local SPD_SLIDER_MIN, SPD_SLIDER_MAX = 15, 100

    -- KACHOW MODE state (shared with the :kachow command in the command engine)
    local kachowModeOn = false
    local kachowSpeed = 50
    local kachowBaseWalk = 16
    local kachowOverride = false
    local kachowBoosting = false

    local function kachowHumanoid()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end
    local function kachowApplySpeed(v)
        local hum = kachowHumanoid()
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = v end
    end
    local function kachowRestore()
        kachowApplySpeed(kachowBaseWalk)
    end

    -- The speed section (header + slider + presets) is always visible; only the
    -- Kachow branding (header / button / context) is hidden until the kachow command.
    local SpdSpeedSection = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 54), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 4),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = SpdView,
    })

    HUB.collapsibleHeader(SpdSpeedSection, "SPEED (" .. SPD_SLIDER_MIN .. "-" .. SPD_SLIDER_MAX .. ")", 0, SpdView)

    local SpdKachowHeader = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 66),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "KACHOW", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.accent, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 15, Visible = false, Parent = SpdView,
    })

    local SpdSliderTrack, SpdFill, SpdKnob, SpdValue
    local function spdApplyValue(v, live)
        v = math.clamp(math.round(v), SPD_SLIDER_MIN, SPD_SLIDER_MAX)
        kachowSpeed = v
        local frac = (v - SPD_SLIDER_MIN) / (SPD_SLIDER_MAX - SPD_SLIDER_MIN)
        if SpdKnob then SpdKnob.Position = UDim2.new(frac, -8, 0.5, -8) end
        if SpdFill then SpdFill.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0) end
        if SpdValue then SpdValue.Text = tostring(v) end
        if live then
            kachowApplySpeed(kachowSpeed)
            -- REDESIGN [SPEED WATCH]: arm the absolute speed watchdog so a
            -- game-side WalkSpeed reset gets re-applied by the boot heartbeat.
            HUB.speedWatchTarget = kachowSpeed
            if kachowModeOn and not kachowBoosting then
                kachowBaseWalk = kachowSpeed
            end
        end
    end

    local function spdSetSpeed(v)
        spdApplyValue(v, true)
        -- REDESIGN [SPEED WATCH]: presets + slider are absolute client
        -- speed control -- arm the watchdog so a game-side WalkSpeed
        -- reset gets re-applied (the heartbeat in BOOT enforces it).
        HUB.speedWatchTarget = v
    end

    SpdSliderTrack = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 16),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Parent = SpdSpeedSection,
    })
    round(SpdSliderTrack, 4)
    stroke(SpdSliderTrack, COLOR.border, 1)
    SpdFill = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent, Parent = SpdSliderTrack,
    })
    round(SpdFill, 3)
    SpdKnob = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -8),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.text, Parent = SpdSliderTrack, ZIndex = 2,
    })
    round(SpdKnob, 8)
    stroke(SpdKnob, COLOR.accent, 2)
    SpdValue = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(1, -41, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "50", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = SpdSpeedSection,
    })
    spdApplyValue(kachowSpeed, false)

    local spdDragging = false
    local function spdUpdateFromMouse()
        local abs = SpdSliderTrack.AbsolutePosition
        local w = SpdSliderTrack.AbsoluteSize.X
        if w <= 0 then return end
        local frac = (UserInputService:GetMouseLocation().X - abs.X) / w
        spdApplyValue(SPD_SLIDER_MIN + (SPD_SLIDER_MAX - SPD_SLIDER_MIN) * math.clamp(frac, 0, 1), true)
    end
    SpdSliderTrack.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            spdUpdateFromMouse()
            spdDragging = true
        end
    end)
    SpdKnob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            spdDragging = true
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if spdDragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            spdUpdateFromMouse()
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then spdDragging = false end
    end)

    -- Same presets as the chase / follow "Follow Speed" dropdown.
    for i, opt in ipairs(SPEED_OPTIONS) do
        local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.2, -7, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.2, (i == 1) and 5 or 2, 0, 32),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = tostring(opt.value), TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = SpdSpeedSection,
        })
        round(b, 5)
        stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
        bindFont(b, "bold")
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            spdSetSpeed(opt.value)
            showToast("Speed: " .. opt.value, "on")
        end)
    end
    -- REDESIGN [SMART REFLOW]: the SPD speed section's content ends at
    -- 54, so collapsing it actually folds the slider + presets away.
    HUB.contentBottom[SpdSpeedSection] = 54

    local SpdModeBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 88),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 45, 45), Text = "KACHOW MODE: OFF", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60),
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Visible = false, Parent = SpdView,
    })
    round(SpdModeBtn, 6)
    stroke(SpdModeBtn, Color3.fromRGB(120, 20, 20), 1)
    bindFont(SpdModeBtn, "bold")

    local SpdTipsLabel = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 120),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "HOLD G TO BOOST \xC2\xB7 H TOGGLES SPEED",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
        Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 10, Visible = false, Parent = SpdView,
    })
    bindFont(SpdTipsLabel, "regular")

    local function kachowReveal()
        SpdKachowHeader.Visible = true
        SpdModeBtn.Visible = true
        SpdTipsLabel.Visible = true
    end

    local function setKachowMode(on)
        if kachowModeOn == on then return end
        kachowModeOn = on
        if on then
            kachowReveal()
            local hum = kachowHumanoid()
            kachowBaseWalk = hum and hum.WalkSpeed or 16
            if kachowOverride then kachowApplySpeed(kachowSpeed) end
        else
kachowOverride = false
    kachowBoosting = false
    kachowRestore()
    -- [TOGGLE WATCHDOG] arm a 3s guard: if WalkSpeed is still boosted past
    -- the base walk speed, restore it.
    if HUB.guardToggleOff then
        HUB.guardToggleOff("speed", function()
            local hum = kachowHumanoid()
            return hum and math.abs((hum.WalkSpeed or 0) - kachowBaseWalk) > 0.01
        end, function()
            kachowOverride = false
            kachowBoosting = false
            kachowRestore()
        end)
    end
        end
        SpdModeBtn.Text = on and "KACHOW MODE: ON" or "KACHOW MODE: OFF"
        showToast(on and "Kachow mode on" or "Kachow mode off", on and "on" or "off")
    end
    SpdModeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setKachowMode(not kachowModeOn)
    end)

    -- G: hold = smooth ramp from base speed (16) up to max (100) over 6.5s at
    -- an even pace (boost, only while Kachow mode is on). H = toggle speed on/off.
    local spdKeyHeld = false
    local spdRampTask = nil
    local SPD_RAMP_DURATION = 6.5

    local function spdStopRamp()
        if spdRampTask then
            spdRampTask:Cancel()
            spdRampTask = nil
        end
    end

    local function spdStartRamp()
        spdStopRamp()
        local startSpeed = kachowBaseWalk
        local targetSpeed = SPD_SLIDER_MAX
        local startTime = os.clock()
        spdRampTask = task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                local t = (os.clock() - startTime) / SPD_RAMP_DURATION
                if t >= 1 then
                    kachowApplySpeed(targetSpeed)
                    break
                end
                kachowApplySpeed(startSpeed + (targetSpeed - startSpeed) * t)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            spdRampTask = nil
        end)
    end

    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.G and kachowModeOn then
            spdKeyHeld = true
            kachowBoosting = true
            spdStartRamp()
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.G and kachowModeOn and spdKeyHeld then
            spdKeyHeld = false
            kachowBoosting = false
            spdStopRamp()
            if not kachowOverride then kachowRestore() end
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.H then
            kachowOverride = not kachowOverride
            if kachowOverride then
                kachowApplySpeed(kachowSpeed)
                showToast("Speed: ON", "on")
            else
                kachowRestore()
                showToast("Speed: OFF", "off")
            end
        end
    end)

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if kachowModeOn and kachowOverride then
            task.wait(0.2)
            kachowApplySpeed(kachowSpeed)
        end
    end)

    kachowApi = {
        setMode = setKachowMode,
        isOn = function() return kachowModeOn end,
    }
end
-- ============================================================
-- PART F3. ESSENTIALS MODULE (state, save/load, core, toggle
-- rows, colors, tools, init -- fully self-contained; nothing
-- outside references its locals). Kept inside its own function
-- so its ~50 locals get a fresh register frame (Luau caps
-- locals per function at 200).
-- ============================================================
local function runEssentials()
local EspView = HUB.views.esp
local EssLight = game:GetService("Lighting")
local EssCam = workspace.CurrentCamera

local EssAmbientSave = EssLight.Ambient
local EssShiftTopSave = EssLight.ColorShift_Top
local EssShiftBotSave = EssLight.ColorShift_Bottom

local EssFolder = newInstance("Folder", { Name = "PSE_Essentials", Parent = game.CoreGui })
local EssPlayerESP = newInstance("Folder", { Name = "PlayerESP", Parent = EssFolder })
local EssItemESP = newInstance("Folder", { Name = "ItemESP", Parent = EssFolder })
local EssNPCESP = newInstance("Folder", { Name = "NPCESP", Parent = EssFolder })
local EssTracerFolder = EssCam and newInstance("Folder", { Name = "Tracers", Parent = EssCam }) or nil

local EssConfig = {
    ESPEnabled = false,
    NPCESPEnabled = false,
    TracersEnabled = false,
    FullbrightEnabled = false,
    CrosshairEnabled = false,
    TracersLength = 500,
    ESPLength = 10000,
    AimAssistEnabled = false,
    AimCircleEnabled = true,
    AimRadius = 150,
    AimStrength = 0.6,
    AimPriority = "nearest",
    HoldToAim = false,
    FarmShooterEnabled = false,
    FireRate = 0.15,
    BoxesEnabled = false,
    FovCircleEnabled = false,
}
local EssColors = {
    Enemy = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70),
    Ally = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 130),
    Crosshair = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70),
    NPC = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0),
}
local EssTracerData = {}
local EssLightHooked = false
local EssCrosshairGui = nil
local EssMyChar = LocalPlayer.Character
local EssNPCList = {}

-- ============================================================
-- ESSENTIALS SAVE / LOAD (V6: in-session snapshots only -- no
-- JSON, no external files; Save/Load work until the hub re-injects)
-- ============================================================
local EssSession = nil

local function EssSaveSettings()
    EssSession = {
        ESPEnabled = EssConfig.ESPEnabled,
        NPCESPEnabled = EssConfig.NPCESPEnabled,
        TracersEnabled = EssConfig.TracersEnabled,
        FullbrightEnabled = EssConfig.FullbrightEnabled,
        CrosshairEnabled = EssConfig.CrosshairEnabled,
        TracersLength = EssConfig.TracersLength,
        ESPLength = EssConfig.ESPLength,
        AimAssistEnabled = EssConfig.AimAssistEnabled,
        AimCircleEnabled = EssConfig.AimCircleEnabled,
        AimRadius = EssConfig.AimRadius,
        AimStrength = EssConfig.AimStrength,
        AimPriority = EssConfig.AimPriority,
        HoldToAim = EssConfig.HoldToAim,
        FarmShooterEnabled = EssConfig.FarmShooterEnabled,
        FireRate = EssConfig.FireRate,
        BoxesEnabled = EssConfig.BoxesEnabled,
        FovCircleEnabled = EssConfig.FovCircleEnabled,
        Colors = {
            Enemy = EssColors.Enemy,
            Ally = EssColors.Ally,
            Crosshair = EssColors.Crosshair,
            NPC = EssColors.NPC,
        },
    }
    showToast("ESP settings saved (session)", "on")
end

local function EssLoadSettings()
    if not EssSession then return end
    for _, key in ipairs({ "ESPEnabled", "NPCESPEnabled", "TracersEnabled", "FullbrightEnabled", "CrosshairEnabled", "AimAssistEnabled", "AimCircleEnabled", "FarmShooterEnabled", "HoldToAim", "BoxesEnabled", "FovCircleEnabled" }) do
        if type(EssSession[key]) == "boolean" then EssConfig[key] = EssSession[key] end
    end
    if type(EssSession.TracersLength) == "number" then EssConfig.TracersLength = EssSession.TracersLength end
    if type(EssSession.ESPLength) == "number" then EssConfig.ESPLength = EssSession.ESPLength end
    if type(EssSession.AimRadius) == "number" then EssConfig.AimRadius = EssSession.AimRadius end
    if type(EssSession.AimStrength) == "number" then EssConfig.AimStrength = EssSession.AimStrength end
    if type(EssSession.FireRate) == "number" then EssConfig.FireRate = EssSession.FireRate end
    if type(EssSession.AimPriority) == "string" then EssConfig.AimPriority = EssSession.AimPriority end
    if EssSession.Colors then
        EssColors.Enemy = EssSession.Colors.Enemy or EssColors.Enemy
        EssColors.Ally = EssSession.Colors.Ally or EssColors.Ally
        EssColors.Crosshair = EssSession.Colors.Crosshair or EssColors.Crosshair
        EssColors.NPC = EssSession.Colors.NPC or EssColors.NPC
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- ESSENTIALS CORE
-- ============================================================
local function EssTeamColor(plr)
    if plr == LocalPlayer then return EssColors.Ally end
    -- Team-smart: same team as the local player = Ally, otherwise Enemy.
    -- Works for 2-team games (Red vs Blue, Killer vs Survivor); falls
    -- back to everyone-else-is-Enemy when no teams are set.
    local myTeam = LocalPlayer.Team
    local theirTeam = plr.Team
    if myTeam ~= nil and theirTeam ~= nil then
        return (myTeam == theirTeam) and EssColors.Ally or EssColors.Enemy
    end
    return EssColors.Enemy
end

local function EssFindESP(obj)
    for _, v in ipairs(EssItemESP:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value == obj then return v.Parent end
    end
    return nil
end

local function EssRemoveESP(obj)
    if obj == nil then return end
    local isPlr = obj:IsA("Player")
    local folder = isPlr and EssPlayerESP or EssItemESP
    local found = isPlr and folder:FindFirstChild("ESP Crap_" .. obj.Name) or EssFindESP(obj)
    if found then found:Destroy() end
end

local function EssCreateESP(obj)
    if obj == nil then return end
    local isPlr = obj:IsA("Player")
    local folder = isPlr and EssPlayerESP or EssItemESP
    local subject = isPlr and obj.Character or obj
    if folder:FindFirstChild("ESP Crap_" .. obj.Name) then return end
    if subject == nil then return end
    local head = subject:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head == nil then return end

    local bb = newInstance("BillboardGui", {
        Adornee = head, ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
        AlwaysOnTop = true, Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5),
        StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0),
        Name = "ESP Crap_" .. obj.Name, Parent = folder,
    })
    local frame = newInstance("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = bb })
    local col = EssTeamColor(obj)

    local nameLabel = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Names", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 10, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -45),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = obj.Name, TextColor3 = col,
        Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, TextSize = 13, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, Parent = frame,
    })
    if isPlr then
        newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Dist", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 10, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -35),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextColor3 = col,
            Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, TextSize = 13, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, Parent = frame,
        })
        newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Health", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 10, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -25),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextColor3 = col,
            Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, TextSize = 13, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, Parent = frame,
        })
    else
        newInstance("ObjectValue", { Value = obj, Parent = bb })
    end
end

local function EssUpdateESP(plr)
    if plr == nil then return end
    local esp = EssPlayerESP:FindFirstChild("ESP Crap_" .. plr.Name)
    if not esp then return end
    local frame = esp.Frame
    local col = EssTeamColor(plr)
    frame.Names.TextColor3 = col
    frame.Dist.TextColor3 = col
    frame.Health.TextColor3 = col

    local char = plr.Character
    local myChar = EssMyChar
    if not (myChar and char) then return end
    local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (myHRP and hrp) then return end

    local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
    local hidden = dist > EssConfig.ESPLength
    frame.Names.Visible = not hidden
    frame.Dist.Visible = not hidden
    frame.Health.Visible = not hidden
    frame.Dist.Text = "Distance: " .. string.format("%.0f", dist)
    frame.Health.Text = hum and ("Health: " .. string.format("%.0f", hum.Health)) or ""
end

local function EssNpcIsNpc(v)
    return v:IsA("Model") and (v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v)
end

local function EssApplyNPC(model)
    if model == nil or not model.Parent then return end
    if model:FindFirstChild("GhostNPC") then return end
    newInstance("Highlight", {
        Name = "GhostNPC", FillColor = EssColors.NPC, OutlineColor = EssColors.NPC,
        FillTransparency = 0.5, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, Parent = model,
    })
    table.insert(EssNPCList, model)
end

local function EssRefreshNPCs()
    if not EssConfig.NPCESPEnabled then return end
    for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if EssNpcIsNpc(v) then EssApplyNPC(v) end
    end
end

local function EssClearNPCs()
    for _, model in ipairs(EssNPCList) do
        local hl = model:FindFirstChild("GhostNPC")
        if hl then hl:Destroy() end
    end
    EssNPCList = {}
end

local function EssUpdateNPCHighlights()
    for _, model in ipairs(EssNPCList) do
        local hl = model:FindFirstChild("GhostNPC")
        if hl then
            hl.FillColor, hl.OutlineColor = EssColors.NPC, EssColors.NPC
        end
    end
end

-- Player body-highlight mapped onto the ESP toggle.
local function EssApplyPlayerHighlight(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    if not char or not char.Parent then return end
    if char:FindFirstChild("GhostESP") then return end
    newInstance("Highlight", {
        Name = "GhostESP", FillColor = EssTeamColor(plr), OutlineColor = EssTeamColor(plr),
        FillTransparency = 0.5, DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, Parent = char,
    })
end

local function EssRemovePlayerHighlight(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    local h = char and char:FindFirstChild("GhostESP")
    if h then h:Destroy() end
end

local function EssUpdatePlayerHighlights()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            local h = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("GhostESP")
            if h then
                local col = EssTeamColor(plr)
                h.FillColor, h.OutlineColor = col, col
            end
        end
    end
end

local function EssClearPlayerHighlights()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then EssRemovePlayerHighlight(plr) end
    end
end

workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(v)
    if EssConfig.NPCESPEnabled and EssNpcIsNpc(v) then EssApplyNPC(v) end
end)

local function EssCreateTracers(plr)
    if EssTracerData[plr.Name] or not EssTracerFolder then return end
    local part = newInstance("Part", {
        Name = plr.Name, Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Transparency = 1,
        Anchored = true, Locked = true, CanCollide = false, Parent = EssTracerFolder,
    })
    local box = newInstance("BoxHandleAdornment", {
        Adornee = part, Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), AlwaysOnTop = true,
        ZIndex = 5, Transparency = 0, Color3 = EssTeamColor(plr), Parent = part,
    })
    local char = plr.Character
    EssTracerData[plr.Name] = {
        Part = part, Box = box,
        HRP = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or nil,
    }
end

local function EssRemoveTracers(plr)
    local d = EssTracerData[plr.Name]
    if d then
        d.Part:Destroy()
        EssTracerData[plr.Name] = nil
    end
end

local function EssUpdateTracer(plr, d)
    if not EssConfig.TracersEnabled then return end
    local myChar = EssMyChar
    if not myChar then return end
    local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hrp = d.HRP
    if not (myHRP and hrp and hrp.Parent) then return end

    local col = EssTeamColor(plr)
    local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
    local inVehicle = myChar:FindFirstChild("InVehicle") or hrp.Parent:FindFirstChild("InVehicle")
    if dist < EssConfig.TracersLength and not inVehicle and EssCam then
        local ray = EssCam:ScreenPointToRay(EssCam.ViewportSize.X / 2, EssCam.ViewportSize.Y, 0)
        local len = (ray.Origin - (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))).Magnitude
        d.Part.Transparency = 1
        d.Part.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, len)
        d.Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(ray.Origin, (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0))) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -len / 2)
        d.Part.BrickColor = BrickColor.new(col)
        d.Box.Transparency = 0
        d.Box.Size = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, len)
        d.Box.Color3 = col
    else
        d.Part.Transparency = 1
        d.Box.Transparency = 1
    end
end

local function EssLightingHax()
    if EssConfig.FullbrightEnabled then
        EssLight.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        EssLight.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        EssLight.ColorShift_Top = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    end
end

local function EssCreateCrosshair()
    if EssCrosshairGui then return end
    local g = newInstance("ScreenGui", { Name = "PSE_Crosshair", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, Parent = game.CoreGui })
    local function line(x, y, w, h)
        newInstance("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, y),
            BackgroundColor3 = EssColors.Crosshair, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 10, Parent = g,
        })
    end
    line(-12, -1, 8, 2)
    line(4, -1, 8, 2)
    line(-1, -12, 2, 8)
    line(-1, 4, 2, 8)
    EssCrosshairGui = g
end

local function EssUpdateCrosshairColor()
    if EssCrosshairGui then
        for _, v in ipairs(EssCrosshairGui:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Frame") then v.BackgroundColor3 = EssColors.Crosshair end
        end
    end
end

local function EssRegisterCharEvents(plr)
    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait()
        if EssConfig.ESPEnabled then
            EssRemoveESP(plr)
            EssCreateESP(plr)
        end
        if EssConfig.TracersEnabled then
            local d = EssTracerData[plr.Name]
            if d then d.HRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
        end
    end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
    if plr ~= LocalPlayer then EssRegisterCharEvents(plr) end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    EssRemoveESP(plr)
    EssRemoveTracers(plr)
    EssRemovePlayerHighlight(plr)
end)

local function EssStatus(label, isOn)
    label.Text = isOn and "On" or "Off"
    label.TextColor3 = isOn and COLOR.on or COLOR.off
end

-- ============================================================
-- ESSENTIALS TOGGLE ROWS (ESP section)
-- ============================================================
HUB.collapsibleHeader(EspView, "ESP", 4)
local EssEspStatus, EssEspSet, EssEspGet, EssEspCB = createSwitchRow(EspView, 22, "ESP", nil, nil, "check")
EssEspCB(function(on)
    EssConfig.ESPEnabled = on
    EssStatus(EssEspStatus, on)
    if on then
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                EssRegisterCharEvents(plr)
                EssRemoveESP(plr)
                EssCreateESP(plr)
                EssApplyPlayerHighlight(plr)
            end
        end
    else
        EssPlayerESP:ClearAllChildren()
        EssItemESP:ClearAllChildren()
        EssClearPlayerHighlights()
    end
end)

local EssTracerStatus, EssTracerSet, EssTracerGet, EssTracerCB = createSwitchRow(EspView, 50, "Tracers", nil, nil, "check")
EssTracerCB(function(on)
    EssConfig.TracersEnabled = on
    EssStatus(EssTracerStatus, on)
    if on then
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                EssRegisterCharEvents(plr)
                if plr.Character then
                    local d = EssTracerData[plr.Name]
                    if d then d.HRP = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
                end
                EssRemoveTracers(plr)
                EssCreateTracers(plr)
            end
        end
    else
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do EssRemoveTracers(plr) end
    end
end)

local EssFullbrightStatus, EssFullbrightSet, EssFullbrightGet, EssFullbrightCB = createSwitchRow(EspView, 78, "Fullbright", nil, nil, "check")
EssFullbrightCB(function(on)
    EssConfig.FullbrightEnabled = on
    EssStatus(EssFullbrightStatus, on)
    if on then
        if not EssLightHooked then
            EssLightHooked = true
            EssLight.Changed:Connect(EssLightingHax)
        end
        EssLightingHax()
    else
        EssLight.Ambient = EssAmbientSave
        EssLight.ColorShift_Bottom = EssShiftBotSave
        EssLight.ColorShift_Top = EssShiftTopSave
    end
end)

local EssCrossStatus, EssCrossSet, EssCrossGet, EssCrossCB = createSwitchRow(EspView, 106, "Crosshair", nil, nil, "check")
EssCrossCB(function(on)
    EssConfig.CrosshairEnabled = on
    EssStatus(EssCrossStatus, on)
    if on then EssCreateCrosshair() else
        if EssCrosshairGui then
            EssCrosshairGui:Destroy()
            EssCrosshairGui = nil
        end
    end
end)

local EssNpcStatus, EssNpcSet, EssNpcGet, EssNpcCB = createSwitchRow(EspView, 134, "NPC ESP", nil, nil, "check")
EssNpcCB(function(on)
    EssConfig.NPCESPEnabled = on
    EssStatus(EssNpcStatus, on)
    if on then
        EssRefreshNPCs()
    else
        EssClearNPCs()
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- ESSENTIALS COLORS (moved into the ESP view, under the Crosshair toggle)
-- ============================================================
local function EssColorButton(parent, y, label, getter, onPick)
    local btn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, y),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = label .. ColorPaletteName(getter()), TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 12, Parent = parent,
    })
    round(btn, 6)
    bindThemed(stroke(btn, COLOR.border, 1), "Color", "border")
    bindFont(btn, "regular")
    createColorPicker(btn, function(o)
        onPick(o)
        btn.Text = label .. ColorPaletteName(getter())
        showToast("Color updated", "on")
    end, getter)
    return btn
end

HUB.collapsibleHeader(EspView, "COLORS", 162)
local EssCrossBtn = EssColorButton(EspView, 182, "Crosshair: ", function()
    return EssColors.Crosshair
end, function(col)
    EssColors.Crosshair = col
    EssUpdateCrosshairColor()
end)
local EssEnemyBtn = EssColorButton(EspView, 210, "Enemy: ", function()
    return EssColors.Enemy
end, function(col)
    EssColors.Enemy = col
    EssUpdatePlayerHighlights()
end)
local EssAllyBtn = EssColorButton(EspView, 238, "Ally: ", function()
    return EssColors.Ally
end, function(col)
    EssColors.Ally = col
    EssUpdatePlayerHighlights()
end)
local EssNpcBtn = EssColorButton(EspView, 266, "NPC: ", function()
    return EssColors.NPC
end, function(col)
    EssColors.NPC = col
    EssUpdateNPCHighlights()
end)

-- ============================================================
-- ESP TOOLS (Save / Load / Reset, moved from the Settings tab)
-- ============================================================
HUB.collapsibleHeader(EspView, "TOOLS", 292)
local EssSaveBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
    Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 308),
    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Save Settings", TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
    Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = EspView,
})
round(EssSaveBtn, 6)
stroke(EssSaveBtn, COLOR.border, 1)
bindFont(EssSaveBtn, "bold")
EssSaveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(EssSaveSettings)

local EssLoadBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
    Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 308),
    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Load Settings", TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
    Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = EspView,
})
round(EssLoadBtn, 6)
stroke(EssLoadBtn, COLOR.border, 1)
bindFont(EssLoadBtn, "bold")
EssLoadBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    EssLoadSettings()
    EssEspSet(EssConfig.ESPEnabled)
    EssTracerSet(EssConfig.TracersEnabled)
    EssFullbrightSet(EssConfig.FullbrightEnabled)
    EssCrossSet(EssConfig.CrosshairEnabled)
    EssNpcSet(EssConfig.NPCESPEnabled)
    EssConfig.AimAssistSet(EssConfig.AimAssistEnabled)
    EssConfig.AimCircleSet(EssConfig.AimCircleEnabled)
    EssConfig.FarmShooterSet(EssConfig.FarmShooterEnabled)
    EssConfig.HoldAimSet(EssConfig.HoldToAim)
    EssConfig.BoxesSet(EssConfig.BoxesEnabled)
    EssConfig.FovSet(EssConfig.FovCircleEnabled)
    EssConfig.updateAimCircle()
    EssEnemyBtn.Text = "Enemy: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.Enemy)
    EssAllyBtn.Text = "Ally: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.Ally)
    EssCrossBtn.Text = "Crosshair: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.Crosshair)
    EssNpcBtn.Text = "NPC: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.NPC)
    showToast("ESP settings loaded", "on")
end)

local EssResetBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 334),
    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Reset to Defaults", TextColor3 = COLOR.warn,
    Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = EspView,
})
round(EssResetBtn, 6)
stroke(EssResetBtn, COLOR.warn, 1)
bindFont(EssResetBtn, "bold")
EssResetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    EssEspSet(false)
    EssTracerSet(false)
    EssFullbrightSet(false)
    EssCrossSet(false)
    EssNpcSet(false)
    EssConfig.AimAssistSet(false)
    EssConfig.AimCircleSet(true)
    EssConfig.AimRadius = 150
    EssConfig.AimStrength = 0.6
    HUB.buttons.aimRadius.Text = "Aim Radius: 150"
    HUB.buttons.aimStrength.Text = "Aim Strength: 0.6"
    EssConfig.FarmShooterSet(false)
    EssConfig.FireRate = 0.15
    HUB.buttons.fireRate.Text = "Fire Rate: 0.15"
    EssConfig.HoldAimSet(false)
    EssConfig.AimPriority = "nearest"
    HUB.buttons.aimPriority.Text = "Aim Priority: Nearest"
    EssConfig.BoxesSet(false)
    EssConfig.FovSet(false)
    EssConfig.updateAimCircle()
    EssColors.Enemy = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
    EssColors.Ally = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 130)
    EssColors.Crosshair = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
    EssColors.NPC = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
    EssEnemyBtn.Text = "Enemy: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.Enemy)
    EssAllyBtn.Text = "Ally: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.Ally)
    EssCrossBtn.Text = "Crosshair: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.Crosshair)
    EssNpcBtn.Text = "NPC: " .. ColorPaletteName(EssColors.NPC)
    showToast("ESP settings reset", "on")
end)

-- ============================================================
-- V7 AIM ASSIST (auto-aim toward the nearest target + radius circle)
-- REDESIGN [MOVE]: built into the SERVER sub-tab (HUB.views.server) at
-- ESP-era coordinates; the shift pass at the end of the server build
-- slides the whole section down to sit right below the server content.
-- ============================================================
HUB.collapsibleHeader(HUB.views.server, "AIM", 362)
do
    local AimAssistStatus, AimAssistSet, _, AimAssistCB = createSwitchRow(HUB.views.server, 380, "Aim Assist", nil)

    -- Radius circle overlay (screen-center ring at the aim radius).
    local AimGui = newInstance("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "PSE_Aim", Parent = game.CoreGui, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    })
    local AimCircle = newInstance("Frame", {
        Name = "AimCircle", Size = UDim2.new(0, EssConfig.AimRadius * 2, 0, EssConfig.AimRadius * 2),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -EssConfig.AimRadius, 0.5, -EssConfig.AimRadius),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 48,
        Visible = false, Parent = AimGui,
    })
    local AimCorner = round(AimCircle, EssConfig.AimRadius)
    stroke(AimCircle, EssColors.Crosshair, 2)

    local function updateAimCircle()
        local r = math.max(20, EssConfig.AimRadius)
        AimCircle.Size = UDim2.new(0, r * 2, 0, r * 2)
        AimCircle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -r, 0.5, -r)
        AimCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)
        AimCircle.Visible = EssConfig.AimCircleEnabled
    end
    updateAimCircle()

    EssConfig.AimAssistSet = AimAssistSet
    EssConfig.updateAimCircle = updateAimCircle

    AimAssistCB(function(on)
        EssConfig.AimAssistEnabled = on
        EssStatus(AimAssistStatus, on)
    end)

    -- Farm Shooter: auto-aim + auto-fire to farm kills.
    local FarmShooterStatus, FarmShooterSet, _, FarmShooterCB = createSwitchRow(HUB.views.server, 408, "Farm Shooter", nil)
    HUB.buttons.fireRate = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 434),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Fire Rate: " .. EssConfig.FireRate,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = HUB.views.server,
    })
    round(HUB.buttons.fireRate, 6)
    stroke(HUB.buttons.fireRate, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(HUB.buttons.fireRate, "bold")
    attachHover(HUB.buttons.fireRate)
    EssConfig.FarmShooterSet = FarmShooterSet
    FarmShooterCB(function(on)
        EssConfig.FarmShooterEnabled = on
        EssStatus(FarmShooterStatus, on)
    end)
    createDropdown(HUB.buttons.fireRate, "Fire Rate: ", {
        {name = "0.05", value = 0.05},
        {name = "0.1", value = 0.1},
        {name = "0.15", value = 0.15},
        {name = "0.2", value = 0.2},
        {name = "0.3", value = 0.3},
        {name = "0.5", value = 0.5},
    }, function(o)
        EssConfig.FireRate = o.value
    end)

    -- Cycle mode / Hold To Aim / Aim Radius / Aim Strength / Aim Priority
    -- moved to Settings > AIM BOT SETTINGS (deep config lives there).

    local lastFire = 0

    -- Line-of-sight: raycast from the camera; a hit before the target
    -- means a wall is in the way (skip the target).
    local function aimVisible(targetPos)
        local camPos = EssCam.CFrame.Position
        local dir = targetPos - camPos
        local dist = dir.Magnitude
        if dist < 0.01 then return true end
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(camPos, dir.Unit * dist, params)
        return hit == nil or (hit.Position - camPos).Magnitude >= dist - 1
    end

    -- Cycle mode: Tab switches the locked aim target (no teleport).
    local function cycleAimTarget()
        if not HUB.cycleMode then return end
        local list = {}
        local myTeam = LocalPlayer.Team
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                local theirTeam = plr.Team
                if not (myTeam ~= nil and theirTeam ~= nil and myTeam == theirTeam) then
                    local char = plr.Character
                    local part = (char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                    if part and aimVisible(part.Position) then table.insert(list, plr) end
                end
            end
        end
        if #list == 0 then showToast("No visible target", "warn") return end
        local idx = 0
        if HUB.aimLockTarget then
            for i, plr in ipairs(list) do
                if plr == HUB.aimLockTarget then idx = i break end
            end
        end
        HUB.aimLockTarget = list[idx % #list + 1]
        showToast("Aim target: " .. HUB.aimLockTarget.Name, "on")
    end
    HUB.cycleAimTarget = cycleAimTarget

    -- Auto-aim toward the locked target (or nearest visible on-screen
    -- target). Farm Shooter forces aim + auto-fires.
    RunService:BindToRenderStep("PSE_Aim_Update", Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value, function()
        local aimActive = EssConfig.AimAssistEnabled or EssConfig.FarmShooterEnabled
        if EssConfig.HoldToAim and not EssConfig.FarmShooterEnabled then
            aimActive = aimActive and UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2)
        end
        if not aimActive then return end
        local cam = EssCam
        local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
        local centerX, centerY = viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2
        local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
        local myTeam = LocalPlayer.Team

        local function aimPart(plr)
            local char = plr.Character
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            return head or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        end

        -- Prefer the locked target while it is still valid + visible.
        local target, targetPart = nil, nil
        if HUB.aimLockTarget and HUB.aimLockTarget.Parent then
            local p = aimPart(HUB.aimLockTarget)
            if p and aimVisible(p.Position) then
                target, targetPart = HUB.aimLockTarget, p
            end
        end
        if not target then
            local bestDist = EssConfig.AimRadius
            local bestHealth = math.huge
            local lowestHP = (EssConfig.AimPriority == "lowest")
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local theirTeam = plr.Team
                    if not (myTeam ~= nil and theirTeam ~= nil and myTeam == theirTeam) then
                        local p = aimPart(plr)
                        if p and aimVisible(p.Position) then
                            local vp = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(p.Position)
                            if vp and vp.Z > 0 then
                                local dx, dy = vp.X - centerX, vp.Y - centerY
                                local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                                if dist <= EssConfig.AimRadius then
                                    if lowestHP then
                                        local hum = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                        local hp = hum and hum.Health or math.huge
                                        if hp < bestHealth then bestHealth = hp; target, targetPart = plr, p end
                                    elseif dist < bestDist then
                                        bestDist = dist; target, targetPart = plr, p
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if target and EssConfig.AimAssistEnabled then HUB.aimLockTarget = target end
        end
        if not target or not targetPart then return end

        local targetDir = (targetPart.Position - camPos).Unit
        local strength = math.clamp(EssConfig.AimStrength, 0, 1)
        local newDir = cam.CFrame.LookVector:Lerp(targetDir, strength).Unit
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, camPos + newDir)

        if EssConfig.FarmShooterEnabled then
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastFire >= EssConfig.FireRate then
                lastFire = now
                local tool = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                if tool then pcall(function() tool:Activate() end) end
            end
        end
    end)
end


-- ============================================================
-- V7 (merged from visuals variant): 2D BOXES + FOV CIRCLE overlays
-- REDESIGN [MOVE]: moved to the SERVER sub-tab with the AIM section.
-- ============================================================
HUB.collapsibleHeader(HUB.views.server, "OVERLAYS", 630)
do
    local EssBoxStatus, EssBoxSet, _, EssBoxCB = createSwitchRow(HUB.views.server, 646, "2D Boxes", nil, nil, "check")
    local EssFovStatus, EssFovSet, _, EssFovCB = createSwitchRow(HUB.views.server, 672, "FOV Circle", nil, nil, "check")
    EssConfig.BoxesSet = EssBoxSet
    EssConfig.FovSet = EssFovSet

    local BoxGui = newInstance("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "PSE_Boxes", Parent = game.CoreGui, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    })
    local BoxFrames = {}
    local FovCircle = newInstance("Frame", {
        Name = "FovCircle", Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 200),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -100, 0.5, -100),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 49,
        Visible = false, Parent = BoxGui,
    })
    round(FovCircle, 100)
    stroke(FovCircle, EssColors.Crosshair, 2)

    local function boxEnsure(plr)
        if BoxFrames[plr.Name] then return BoxFrames[plr.Name] end
        local f = newInstance("Frame", {
            Name = "Box_" .. plr.Name, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 50, Visible = false, Parent = BoxGui,
        })
        stroke(f, EssTeamColor(plr), 2)
        BoxFrames[plr.Name] = f
        return f
    end
    local function boxRemove(plr)
        local f = BoxFrames[plr.Name]
        if f then f:Destroy() BoxFrames[plr.Name] = nil end
    end

    EssBoxCB(function(on)
        EssConfig.BoxesEnabled = on
        EssStatus(EssBoxStatus, on)
        if not on then
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do boxRemove(plr) end
        end
    end)
    EssFovCB(function(on)
        EssConfig.FovCircleEnabled = on
        EssStatus(EssFovStatus, on)
        FovCircle.Visible = on
    end)

    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(boxRemove)

    RunService:BindToRenderStep("PSE_Boxes_Update", Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value, function()
        if not EssConfig.BoxesEnabled then return end
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                local char = plr.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local f = boxEnsure(plr)
                if not hrp then
                    f.Visible = false
                else
                    local vp = EssCam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                    if not vp or vp.Z <= 0 then
                        f.Visible = false
                    else
                        local topY = vp.Y
                        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if head then
                            local tvp = EssCam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                            if tvp and tvp.Z > 0 then topY = tvp.Y end
                        end
                        local height = math.max(24, vp.Y - topY)
                        local width = height * 0.5
                        f.Position = UDim2.new(0, vp.X - width / 2, 0, topY)
                        f.Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, height)
                        f.Visible = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- AIM BOT SETTINGS (Settings page): the deep aim configuration
-- lives here under its own collapsible header; the Server AIM
-- section keeps only the quick toggles (Aim Assist / Farm
-- Shooter / Fire Rate) + the radius overlay. UI PRESETS stay put.
-- ============================================================
do
    local aimbotY = 0
    for _, child in ipairs(SettingsPage:GetChildren()) do
        local py = child.Position and child.Position.Y
        local cy = py and py.Offset
        local sy = child.Size and child.Size.Y
        local ch = sy and sy.Offset or 0
        if cy and cy + ch > aimbotY then aimbotY = cy + ch end
    end
    aimbotY = aimbotY + 10
    HUB.collapsibleHeader(SettingsPage, "AIM BOT SETTINGS", aimbotY, SettingsPage)
    aimbotY = aimbotY + 14 + 6

    local function aimRow() local r = aimbotY aimbotY = aimbotY + 30 return r end

    -- Aim Circle (radius ring overlay toggle).

    AimCircleCB(function(on)
        EssConfig.AimCircleEnabled = on
        EssStatus(AimCircleStatus, on)
        if EssConfig.updateAimCircle then EssConfig.updateAimCircle() end
    end)
    EssConfig.AimCircleSet = function(on)
        AimCircleSet(on)
        setRowStatus(AimCircleStatus, on)

    -- Aim Radius.

        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, aimRow()),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Aim Radius: " .. EssConfig.AimRadius,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = SettingsPage,
    })
    round(HUB.buttons.aimRadius, 6)
    stroke(HUB.buttons.aimRadius, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(HUB.buttons.aimRadius, "bold")
    attachHover(HUB.buttons.aimRadius)
    createDropdown(HUB.buttons.aimRadius, "Aim Radius: ", {
        {name = "25", value = 25},
        {name = "50", value = 50},
        {name = "75", value = 75},
        {name = "100", value = 100},
        {name = "150", value = 150},
        {name = "200", value = 200},
        {name = "250", value = 250},
        {name = "300", value = 300},
        {name = "400", value = 400},
        {name = "500", value = 500},
    }, function(o)
        EssConfig.AimRadius = o.value
        if EssConfig.updateAimCircle then EssConfig.updateAimCircle() end
    end)

    -- Aim Strength.
    HUB.buttons.aimStrength = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, aimRow()),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Aim Strength: " .. EssConfig.AimStrength,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = SettingsPage,
    })
    round(HUB.buttons.aimStrength, 6)
    stroke(HUB.buttons.aimStrength, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(HUB.buttons.aimStrength, "bold")
    attachHover(HUB.buttons.aimStrength)
    createDropdown(HUB.buttons.aimStrength, "Aim Strength: ", {
        {name = "0.1", value = 0.1},
        {name = "0.2", value = 0.2},
        {name = "0.3", value = 0.3},
        {name = "0.4", value = 0.4},
        {name = "0.5", value = 0.5},
        {name = "0.6", value = 0.6},
        {name = "0.7", value = 0.7},
        {name = "0.8", value = 0.8},
        {name = "0.9", value = 0.9},
        {name = "1.0", value = 1.0},
    }, function(o)
        EssConfig.AimStrength = o.value
    end)

    -- Cycle Mode (Tab cycles the locked aim target).
    local CycleModeStatus, _, _, CycleModeCB = createSwitchRow(SettingsPage, aimRow(), "Cycle Mode", nil)
    CycleModeCB(function(on)
        HUB.cycleMode = on
        EssStatus(CycleModeStatus, on)
    end)

    -- Hold To Aim (right mouse button held).
    local HoldAimStatus, HoldAimSet, _, HoldAimCB = createSwitchRow(SettingsPage, aimRow(), "Hold To Aim", nil, nil, "check")

        EssConfig.HoldToAim = on

    round(HUB.buttons.aimPriority, 6)
    stroke(HUB.buttons.aimPriority, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(HUB.buttons.aimPriority, "bold")

    createDropdown(HUB.buttons.aimPriority, "Aim Priority: ", {

    for _, kc in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do

    createDropdown(HUB.buttons.aimKey, "Aim Assist Key: ", aimKeyOptions, function(o)

    -- Keep the settings scroll area long enough + track the section bottom.
    local aimBottom = aimbotY + 8
    HUB.contentBottom[SettingsPage] = math.max(HUB.contentBottom[SettingsPage] or 0, aimBottom)
    SettingsPage.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(aimBottom + 8, 1400))
end

    label.Text = isOn and "On" or "Off"
    label.TextColor3 = isOn and COLOR.on or COLOR.off
end

    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
            if EssConfig.ESPEnabled then
                EssCreateESP(plr)
                EssUpdateESP(plr)
                EssApplyPlayerHighlight(plr)
            end
            if EssConfig.TracersEnabled then
                EssCreateTracers(plr)
                local d = EssTracerData[plr.Name]
                if d then pcall(EssUpdateTracer, plr, d) end
            end
        end
    end
    if EssConfig.ESPEnabled then EssUpdatePlayerHighlights() end
end)
