-- ============================================================
-- PART F4. COMMAND ENGINE + TOOLS (console, command registry,
-- movement, stats, camera, utility, mods, jerk, server extras)
-- The registry + exec live at this scope (like the original
-- do-scope) so Part F5 and the main-chunk wiring can call them.
-- ============================================================

-- ============================================================
-- COMMAND REGISTRY + EXEC (do-scope level)
-- ============================================================
local cmds = {}
local function addcmd(name, alias, func, hidden)
    table.insert(cmds, {NAME = name, ALIAS = alias or {}, FUNC = func, HIDDEN = hidden})
end

local function findCmd(name)
    for _, c in ipairs(cmds) do
        if c.NAME == name then return c end
        for _, a in ipairs(c.ALIAS) do
            if a == name then return c end
        end
    end
end

local function notify(text, text2)
    local full = text .. (text2 and (" - " .. text2) or "")
    -- [UNIFY] one pipeline, two surfaces: the command feedback always lands
    -- in the toast pool + notify history AND flashes the live terminal strip
    -- under the bell (so console command results read as terminal output).
    showToast(full, "textDim")
    if HUB.terminalNotify then
        local low = full:lower()
        local key = (low:find("off", 1, true) or low:find("fail", 1, true)
            or low:find("error", 1, true) or low:find("unknown", 1, true)) and "warn" or "ok"
        HUB.terminalNotify.fire(full, key)
    end
end

local function getRoot(char)
    return char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
end

local function isNumber(str)
    return tonumber(str) ~= nil
end

local function splitString(str, delim)
    local escaped = delim:gsub("(%W)", "%%%1")
    local broken = {}
    for w in string.gmatch(str, "[^" .. escaped .. "]+") do
        table.insert(broken, w)
    end
    return broken
end

local function getPlayer(list, speaker)
    if not list then return {speaker} end
    local found = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(splitString(list, ",")) do
        name = name:gsub("^[+-]", "")
        local lower = name:lower()
        if lower == "all" then
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(found, p) end
        elseif lower == "others" then
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= speaker then table.insert(found, p) end
            end
        elseif lower == "me" then
            table.insert(found, speaker)
        elseif lower == "random" then
            local others = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= speaker then table.insert(others, p) end
            end
            if #others > 0 then table.insert(found, others[math.random(1, #others)]) end
        else
            local isDisplay = name:sub(1, 1) == "@"
            local search = isDisplay and name:sub(2) or name
            if search == "" then continue end
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local match = (p.Name:lower():sub(1, #search) == search:lower())
                    or (p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #search) == search:lower())
                if match then table.insert(found, p) end
            end
        end
    end
    return found
end

local function execCmd(cmdStr, speaker)
    cmdStr = cmdStr:gsub("%s+$", "")
    cmdStr = cmdStr:gsub("^%s*", "")
    cmdStr = cmdStr:gsub("^;", "")
    cmdStr = cmdStr:gsub("^/e ", "")
    task.spawn(function()
        local commandsToRun = splitString(cmdStr, "\\")
        for _, v in ipairs(commandsToRun) do
            local _, after, num = v:find("^(%d+)%^")
            local cmdDelay = 0
            local infTimes = false
            if num then
                v = v:sub(after + 1)
                local _, after2, del = v:find("^([%d%.]+)%^")
                if del then
                    v = v:sub(after2 + 1)
                    cmdDelay = tonumber(del) or 0
                end
            else
                local infPos = v:find("^inf%^")
                if infPos then
                    infTimes = true
                    v = v:sub(infPos + 4)
                    local _, after2, del = v:find("^([%d%.]+)%^")
                    if del then
                        v = v:sub(after2 + 1)
                        cmdDelay = (tonumber(del) or 1)
                    else
                        cmdDelay = 1
                    end
                end
            end
            num = tonumber(num or 1)

            local args = splitString(v, " ")
            -- [V6] Empty / whitespace-only commands no longer shout
            -- "Unknown command: nil" into the toast.
            if #args == 0 or args[1] == "" then continue end
            local cmd = findCmd(args[1])
            if cmd then
                table.remove(args, 1)
                if infTimes then
                    while true do
                        pcall(cmd.FUNC, args, speaker)
                        task.wait(cmdDelay)
                    end
                else
                    for rep = 1, num do
                        pcall(cmd.FUNC, args, speaker)
                        if cmdDelay ~= 0 then task.wait(cmdDelay) end
                    end
                end
            else
                showToast("Unknown command: " .. tostring(args[1]), "warn")
            end
        end
    end)
end
HUB.execCmd = execCmd

do
local function runCommandEngine()
local ConsoleView = HUB.views.console
local CmdPage = newInstance("Frame", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 180), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = true, Parent = ConsoleView,
})

local CmdBar = newInstance("TextBox", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 4),
    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Command Bar (;)",
    TextColor3 = COLOR.text, PlaceholderColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
    Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 12, Parent = CmdPage,
})
round(CmdBar, 6)
stroke(CmdBar, COLOR.border, 1)
bindFont(CmdBar, "regular")

local CmdRunBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
    Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -65, 0, 4),
    BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Text = "Run", TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
    Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = CmdPage,
})
round(CmdRunBtn, 6)
bindThemed(CmdRunBtn, "BackgroundColor3", "accent2")
bindFont(CmdRunBtn, "bold")

-- ============================================================
-- MOVEMENT (noclip / fly / swim)
-- ============================================================
local Clip = true
local Noclipping = nil
addcmd("noclip", {}, function(args, speaker)
    Clip = false
    -- [V6] Re-running :noclip no longer stacks Stepped connections.
    if Noclipping then Noclipping:Disconnect() Noclipping = nil end
    local function NoclipLoop()
        if not Clip and speaker.Character then
            for _, child in pairs(speaker.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanCollide == true and child.Name ~= "FlyPart" then
                    child.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
    Noclipping = RunService.Stepped:Connect(NoclipLoop)
    if args[1] and args[1] == "nonotify" then return end
    notify("Noclip", "Noclip Enabled")
end)
addcmd("clip", {"unnoclip"}, function(args, speaker)
    if Noclipping then Noclipping:Disconnect() end
    Clip = true
    -- [TOGGLE WATCHDOG] arm a 3s guard: if the Stepped loop or any
    -- CanCollide=false part is still live after the deadline, force-clean.
    if HUB.guardToggleOff then
        HUB.guardToggleOff("noclip", function()
            if Noclipping then return true end
            local char = speaker.Character
            if char then
                for _, child in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name ~= "FlyPart" and child.CanCollide == false then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end, function()
            if Noclipping then Noclipping:Disconnect() Noclipping = nil end
            Clip = true
            local char = speaker.Character
            if char then
                for _, child in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name ~= "FlyPart" then
                        child.CanCollide = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    if args[1] and args[1] == "nonotify" then return end
    notify("Noclip", "Noclip Disabled")
end)

local FLYING = false
local QEfly = true
local flySpeed = 1
local flyKeyDown, flyKeyUp

local function sFLY()
    repeat task.wait() until LocalPlayer.Character and getRoot(LocalPlayer.Character) and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local flyMouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
    if flyKeyDown or flyKeyUp then flyKeyDown:Disconnect() flyKeyUp:Disconnect() end
    local T = getRoot(LocalPlayer.Character)
    local CONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
    local lCONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
    local SPEED = 0
    local function FLY()
        FLYING = true
        local BG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        BG.P = 9e4
        BG.Parent = T
        BV.Parent = T
        BG.maxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        BG.cframe = T.CFrame
        BV.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        BV.maxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        task.spawn(function()
            repeat task.wait()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum.PlatformStand = true end
                if CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R ~= 0 or CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B ~= 0 or CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E ~= 0 then
                    SPEED = 50
                elseif SPEED ~= 0 then
                    SPEED = 0
                end
                if (CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) ~= 0 then
                    BV.velocity = ((workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B)) + ((workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R, (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).p) - workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p)) * SPEED
                    lCONTROL = {F = CONTROL.F, B = CONTROL.B, L = CONTROL.L, R = CONTROL.R}
                elseif SPEED ~= 0 then
                    BV.velocity = ((workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B)) + ((workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(lCONTROL.L + lCONTROL.R, (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).p) - workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p)) * SPEED
                else
                    BV.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
                BG.cframe = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame
            until not FLYING
            CONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
            lCONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
            SPEED = 0
            BG:Destroy()
            BV:Destroy()
            local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
        end)
    end
    flyKeyDown = flyMouse.KeyDown:Connect(function(KEY)
        local k = KEY:lower()
        if k == "w" then CONTROL.F = flySpeed
        elseif k == "s" then CONTROL.B = -flySpeed
        elseif k == "a" then CONTROL.L = -flySpeed
        elseif k == "d" then CONTROL.R = flySpeed
        elseif QEfly and k == "e" then CONTROL.Q = flySpeed * 2
        elseif QEfly and k == "q" then CONTROL.E = -flySpeed * 2 end
        pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Track end)
    end)
    flyKeyUp = flyMouse.KeyUp:Connect(function(KEY)
        local k = KEY:lower()
        if k == "w" then CONTROL.F = 0
        elseif k == "s" then CONTROL.B = 0
        elseif k == "a" then CONTROL.L = 0
        elseif k == "d" then CONTROL.R = 0
        elseif k == "e" then CONTROL.Q = 0
        elseif k == "q" then CONTROL.E = 0 end
    end)
    FLY()
end

local function NOFLY()
    FLYING = false
    if flyKeyDown or flyKeyUp then flyKeyDown:Disconnect() flyKeyUp:Disconnect() end
    local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
    pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
    -- [TOGGLE WATCHDOG] arm a 3s guard: if the fly flag or a leftover
    -- BodyGyro/BodyVelocity is still on the root, force-clean it.
    if HUB.guardToggleOff then
        HUB.guardToggleOff("fly", function()
            if FLYING then return true end
            local root = getRoot(LocalPlayer.Character)
            if root and (root:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyGyro") or root:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyVelocity")) then
                return true
            end
            return false
        end, function()
            FLYING = false
            if flyKeyDown then flyKeyDown:Disconnect() flyKeyDown = nil end
            if flyKeyUp then flyKeyUp:Disconnect() flyKeyUp = nil end
            local root = getRoot(LocalPlayer.Character)
            if root then
                local bg = root:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyGyro")
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
                local bv = root:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyVelocity")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
            end
            local hm = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hm then hm.PlatformStand = false end
            pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
        end)
    end
end

addcmd("fly", {}, function(args, speaker)
    NOFLY()
    task.wait()
    sFLY()
    if args[1] and isNumber(args[1]) then flySpeed = tonumber(args[1]) end
end)
addcmd("unfly", {"nofly"}, function(args, speaker)
    NOFLY()
end)
addcmd("flyspeed", {"flysp"}, function(args, speaker)
    local speed = args[1] or 1
    if isNumber(speed) then flySpeed = tonumber(speed) end
end)

local swimming = false
local oldgrav = workspace.Gravity
local gravReset = nil
local swimbeat = nil
local function setHumanoidStates(hum, enabled)
    local enums = Enum.HumanoidStateType:GetEnumItems()
    local noneIdx = table.find(enums, Enum.HumanoidStateType.None)
    if noneIdx then table.remove(enums, noneIdx) end
    for _, v in ipairs(enums) do
        if v ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then hum:SetStateEnabled(v, enabled) end
    end
end
addcmd("swim", {}, function(args, speaker)
    if not swimming and speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
        oldgrav = workspace.Gravity
        workspace.Gravity = 0
        local Humanoid = speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        gravReset = Humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            workspace.Gravity = oldgrav
            swimming = false
            if swimbeat then swimbeat:Disconnect() swimbeat = nil end
            setHumanoidStates(Humanoid, true)
        end)
        setHumanoidStates(Humanoid, false)
        Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming)
        swimbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                speaker.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = ((Humanoid.MoveDirection ~= Vector3.new() or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)) and speaker.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity or Vector3.new())
            end)
        end)
        swimming = true
        if args[1] and args[1] == "nonotify" then return end
        notify("Swim", "Swim enabled")
    end
end)
addcmd("unswim", {"noswim"}, function(args, speaker)
    if speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
        workspace.Gravity = oldgrav
        swimming = false
        if gravReset then gravReset:Disconnect() end
        if swimbeat then swimbeat:Disconnect() swimbeat = nil end
        local Humanoid = speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        setHumanoidStates(Humanoid, true)
        if args[1] and args[1] == "nonotify" then return end
        notify("Swim", "Swim disabled")
    end
end)

addcmd("speed", {"ws", "walkspeed"}, function(args, speaker)
    local speed = args[2] or args[1] or 16
    if isNumber(speed) then
        local hum = speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = tonumber(speed) end
    end
end)
addcmd("jumppower", {"jp", "jump"}, function(args, speaker)
    local jp = args[1] or 50
    if isNumber(jp) then
        local hum = speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.JumpPower = tonumber(jp) end
    end
end)
addcmd("god", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local Cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local Pos, Char = Cam.CFrame, speaker.Character
    local Human = Char and Char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
    local nHuman = Human and Human:Clone()
    if not nHuman then return end
    nHuman.Parent, speaker.Character = Char, nil
    nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.None, false)
    nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, false)
    nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
    nHuman.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
    Human:Destroy()
    speaker.Character = Char
    Cam.CameraSubject = nHuman
    task.wait()
    Cam.CFrame = Pos
    nHuman.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
    local Script = Char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if Script then
        Script.Disabled = true
        task.wait()
        Script.Disabled = false
    end
    nHuman.Health = nHuman.MaxHealth
end)

-- ============================================================
-- CAMERA (freecam / fov)
-- ============================================================
local fcRunning = false
local fcPart = nil
local fcConn = nil
local fcYaw, fcPitch = 0, 0

local function startFreecam()
    if fcRunning then return end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    fcRunning = true
    fcYaw, fcPitch = 0, 0
    fcPart = Instance.new("Part")
    fcPart.Name = "Freecam"
    fcPart.Anchored = true
    fcPart.CanCollide = false
    fcPart.Transparency = 1
    fcPart.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    fcPart.Parent = workspace
    fcPart.CFrame = cam.CFrame
    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
    cam.CFrame = fcPart.CFrame
    fcConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local speed = 30 * dt
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then speed = speed * 5 end
        local cf = fcPart.CFrame
        local look, right, up = cf.LookVector, cf.RightVector, cf.UpVector
        local dir = Vector3.new()
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir = dir + look end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir = dir - look end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir = dir - right end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir = dir + right end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then dir = dir + up end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then dir = dir - up end
        if dir.Magnitude > 0 then dir = dir.Unit * speed end
        if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then
            local d = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
            fcYaw = fcYaw - d.X * 0.003
            fcPitch = math.clamp(fcPitch - d.Y * 0.003, -1.5, 1.5)
            fcPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(fcPart.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, fcYaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(fcPitch, 0, 0)
        end
        fcPart.CFrame = fcPart.CFrame + dir
        cam.CFrame = fcPart.CFrame
    end)
    notify("Freecam", "Right-click to look around")
end

local function stopFreecam()
    if not fcRunning then return end
    fcRunning = false
    if fcConn then fcConn:Disconnect() fcConn = nil end
    if fcPart then fcPart:Destroy() fcPart = nil end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end
end

addcmd("freecam", {"fc"}, function(args, speaker)
    startFreecam()
end)
addcmd("unfreecam", {"nofreecam", "unfc", "nofc"}, function(args, speaker)
    stopFreecam()
end)
addcmd("fov", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local fov = args[1] or 70
    if isNumber(fov) then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = tonumber(fov)
        notify("FOV", tostring(workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView))
    end
end)

addcmd("antiafk", {"antiidle"}, function(args, speaker)
    local GC = getconnections or get_signal_cons
    if GC then
        for _, v in pairs(GC(Players.LocalPlayer.Idled)) do
            if v.Disable then
                v.Disable(v)
            elseif v.Disconnect then
                v.Disconnect(v)
            end
        end
    else
        local VirtualUser = cloneref(game:GetService("VirtualUser"))
        Players.LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
            VirtualUser:CaptureController()
            VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        end)
    end
    if not (args[1] and tostring(args[1]) == "nonotify") then notify("Anti Idle", "Anti idle is enabled") end
end)

local tweenSpeed = 1
addcmd("goto", {"to"}, function(args, speaker)
    for _, v in ipairs(getPlayer(args[1], speaker)) do
        if v.Character and speaker.Character then
            local hum = speaker.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.SeatPart then
                hum.Sit = false
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            local root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
            local target = getRoot(v.Character)
            if root and target then
                root.CFrame = target.CFrame + Vector3.new(3, 1, 0)
            end
        end
    end
end)
addcmd("tweengoto", {"tgoto", "tto", "tweento"}, function(args, speaker)
    for _, v in ipairs(getPlayer(args[1], speaker)) do
        if v.Character and speaker.Character then
            local hum = speaker.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.SeatPart then
                hum.Sit = false
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            local root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
            local target = getRoot(v.Character)
            if root and target then
                TweenService:Create(root, TweenInfo.new(tweenSpeed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = target.CFrame + Vector3.new(3, 1, 0)}):Play()
            end
        end
    end
end)
addcmd("tppos", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
    if root then
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(tonumber(args[1]) or root.Position.X, tonumber(args[2]) or root.Position.Y, tonumber(args[3]) or root.Position.Z)
    end
end)
addcmd("offset", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
    if root then
        root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(tonumber(args[1]) or 0, tonumber(args[2]) or 0, tonumber(args[3]) or 0)
    end
end)
addcmd("respawn", {"refresh"}, function(args, speaker)
    local char = speaker.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.None) end
        char:ClearAllChildren()
        local newChar = Instance.new("Model")
        newChar.Parent = workspace
        speaker.Character = newChar
        task.wait()
        speaker.Character = char
        newChar:Destroy()
    end
end)
addcmd("notify", {}, function(args, speaker)
    notify("Notification", table.concat(args, " "))
end)
addcmd("help", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local names = {}
    for _, c in ipairs(cmds) do
        if not c.HIDDEN then table.insert(names, c.NAME) end
    end
    notify("Commands", table.concat(names, ", "))
end)

-- Remote inspection: list + log remotes so you can find a game's
-- teleport/summon remote for real server-side "tp others".
addcmd("remotes", {"rems"}, function(args, speaker)
    local filter = args[1] and args[1]:lower()
    local found = {}
    local roots = { game.ReplicatedStorage, game.Players, workspace }
    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
        if root then
            for _, v in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("RemoteEvent") or v:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    if not filter or v.Name:lower():find(filter, 1, true) then
                        table.insert(found, v:GetFullName())
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    notify("Remotes found", tostring(#found) .. (filter and (" matching '" .. filter .. "'") or ""))
    for _, name in ipairs(found) do print("[REMOTE] " .. name) end
end)
addcmd("spy", {"spyremotes"}, function(args, speaker)
    if HUB.remoteSpyOn then notify("Remote spy already on", nil) return end
    if type(hookfunction) ~= "function" then notify("No hookfunction on this executor", nil) return end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local oldFire = hookfunction(Instance.new("RemoteEvent").FireServer, function(self, ...)
            print("[SPY] FireServer " .. tostring(self.Name) .. " <- " .. tostring(table.concat({...}, ", ")))
            return oldFire(self, ...)
        end)
        local oldInvoke = hookfunction(Instance.new("RemoteFunction").InvokeServer, function(self, ...)
            print("[SPY] InvokeServer " .. tostring(self.Name) .. " <- " .. tostring(table.concat({...}, ", ")))
            return oldInvoke(self, ...)
        end)
        HUB.remoteSpyOn = true
        notify("Remote spy ON", "check console")
    end)
    if not ok then notify("Spy hook failed", nil) end
end)

-- Fire a RemoteEvent by name/path (manual "backdoor" for server-side
-- tp-others etc.). Args are passed as strings; numbers are auto-converted.
addcmd("fire", {"firemote"}, function(args, speaker)
    local path = args[1]
    if not path then notify("Usage: :fire <remote> [args...]", nil) return end
    local remote = nil
    for _, root in ipairs({ game.ReplicatedStorage, game.Players, workspace }) do
        if root then
            for _, v in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("RemoteEvent") and (v.Name == path or v:GetFullName() == path) then
                    remote = v
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if remote then break end
    end
    if not remote then notify("Remote not found", path) return end
    local fireArgs = {}
    for i = 2, #args do
        local a = args[i]
        local num = tonumber(a)
        fireArgs[#fireArgs + 1] = num ~= nil and num or a
    end
    pcall(function() remote:FireServer(unpack(fireArgs)) end)
    notify("Fired " .. remote.Name, #fireArgs > 0 and table.concat(fireArgs, ", ") or "no args")
end)

-- Stat editor: list + edit a player's leaderstats / value objects.
addcmd("stats", {"getstats"}, function(args, speaker)
    local target = getPlayer(args[1], speaker)[1]
    if not target then notify("No player", args[1] or "") return end
    local stats = {}
    local function scan(container)
        if not container then return end
        for _, v in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("IntValue") or v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("StringValue") or v:IsA("BoolValue") then
                table.insert(stats, v.Name .. " = " .. tostring(v.Value))
            end
        end
    end
    scan(target:FindFirstChild("leaderstats"))
    scan(target:FindFirstChild("Character"))
    for _, v in ipairs(target:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("IntValue") or v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("StringValue") or v:IsA("BoolValue") then
            table.insert(stats, v.Name .. " = " .. tostring(v.Value))
        end
    end
    notify(target.Name .. " stats", #stats > 0 and table.concat(stats, ", ") or "none")
    for _, s in ipairs(stats) do print("[STAT] " .. s) end
end)
-- Shared recursive stat finder (leaderstats + Character + direct children).
HUB.findStat = function(target, statName)
    if not target or not statName then return nil end
    local found = nil
    local function find(container)
        if found or not container then return end
        for _, v in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
            if v.Name == statName and (v:IsA("IntValue") or v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("StringValue") or v:IsA("BoolValue")) then
                found = v
                return
            end
        end
    end
    find(target:FindFirstChild("leaderstats"))
    find(target:FindFirstChild("Character"))
    if not found then
        for _, v in ipairs(target:GetChildren()) do
            if v.Name == statName and (v:IsA("IntValue") or v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("StringValue") or v:IsA("BoolValue")) then
                found = v
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return found
end
addcmd("setstat", {"editstat"}, function(args, speaker)
    local target = getPlayer(args[1], speaker)[1]
    local statName = args[2]
    local value = args[3]
    if not target or not statName or value == nil then notify("Usage: :setstat <player> <stat> <value>", nil) return end
    local stat = HUB.findStat(target, statName)
    if not stat then notify("Stat not found", statName) return end
    if stat:IsA("IntValue") then stat.Value = math.floor(tonumber(value) or 0)
    elseif stat:IsA("NumberValue") then stat.Value = tonumber(value) or 0
    elseif stat:IsA("StringValue") then stat.Value = tostring(value)
    elseif stat:IsA("BoolValue") then stat.Value = (value == "true" or value == "1")
    end
    notify("Set " .. statName, tostring(stat.Value))
end)
-- Watch a stat: prints value changes to the console every second.
addcmd("statwatch", {"sw", "watchstat"}, function(args, speaker)
    local target = getPlayer(args[1], speaker)[1]
    local statName = args[2]
    if not target or not statName then notify("Usage: :statwatch <player> <stat>", nil) return end
    if HUB.statWatchTask then task.cancel(HUB.statWatchTask) HUB.statWatchTask = nil end
    local stat = HUB.findStat(target, statName)
    if not stat then notify("Stat not found", statName) return end
    local last = stat.Value
    notify("Watching " .. statName, target.Name)
    print("[WATCH] " .. statName .. " = " .. tostring(last))
    HUB.statWatchTask = task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1)
            local s = HUB.findStat(target, statName)
            if not s then print("[WATCH] " .. statName .. " removed") return end
            if s.Value ~= last then
                print("[WATCH] " .. statName .. ": " .. tostring(last) .. " -> " .. tostring(s.Value))
                last = s.Value
            end
        end
    end)
end)
addcmd("statwatchoff", {"swo", "unwatchstat"}, function(args, speaker)
    if HUB.statWatchTask then task.cancel(HUB.statWatchTask) HUB.statWatchTask = nil end
    notify("Watch stopped", nil)
end)

addcmd("kachow", {"km"}, function(args, speaker)
    kachowApi.setMode(not kachowApi.isOn())
end, true)

-- ============================================================
-- MODS (invisible / fling / superring / decal / hint / music)
-- ============================================================
local function loadRemoteScript(url)
    return pcall(function()
        loadstring(game:HttpGet(url, true))()
    end)
end

local invisOn = false
local invisOffset = -100
local function setInvisState(state)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if o.Name == "invischair" then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
    end
    for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then v.Transparency = 0 end
    end
    if state then
        local r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not r then return end
        task.wait(0.05)
        local scf = r.CFrame
        local under = r.Position + Vector3.new(0, invisOffset, 0)
        char:MoveTo(under)
        task.wait(0.1)
        local s = newInstance("Seat", { Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, Transparency = 1, Position = under, Name = "invischair", Parent = workspace })
        newInstance("Weld", { Part0 = s, Part1 = r, Parent = s })
        task.wait()
        s.CFrame = scf
        s.Anchored = false
        for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then v.Transparency = 0.5 end
        end
    end
    invisOn = state
end

addcmd("invisible", {}, function(args, speaker)
    setInvisState(not invisOn)
end)

-- ============================================================
-- SERVER TAB EXTRAS (invis offset slider, FPS boost, unlock jump)
-- ============================================================
local function runServerTabExtras()
-- Invisible offset: depth under the floor, driven by the Server
-- tab slider + presets.
local INVIS_OPTIONS = {
    {name = "Shallow (25)",  value = 25},
    {name = "Default (100)", value = 100},
    {name = "Deep (250)",    value = 250},
    {name = "Extreme (400)", value = 400},
    {name = "Void (600)",    value = 600},
}
local INVIS_SLIDER_MIN, INVIS_SLIDER_MAX = 25, 600
local InvisSliderTrack, InvisFill, InvisKnob, InvisValue
local function invisApplyValue(v, live)
    v = math.clamp(math.round(v), INVIS_SLIDER_MIN, INVIS_SLIDER_MAX)
    invisOffset = -v
    local frac = (v - INVIS_SLIDER_MIN) / (INVIS_SLIDER_MAX - INVIS_SLIDER_MIN)
    if InvisKnob then InvisKnob.Position = UDim2.new(frac, -8, 0.5, -8) end
    if InvisFill then InvisFill.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0) end
    if InvisValue then InvisValue.Text = tostring(v) end
end
local function invisSetDepth(v)
    invisApplyValue(v, true)
end

-- FPS boost + unlock jump, wired to both the Server tab toggles and
-- the :fpsboost / :unjump commands.
local fpsBoostOn = false
local fpsCache = {}
local fpsLightingCache = {}
local function setFpsBoost(state)
    fpsBoostOn = state
    local light = game.Lighting
    if state then
        fpsLightingCache = { GS = light.GlobalShadows, FE = light.FogEnd }
        light.GlobalShadows = false
        light.FogEnd = 9e9
        for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v:IsA("Terrain") then
                if not fpsCache[v] then
                    fpsCache[v] = { M = v.Material, R = v.Reflectance, C = v.CastShadow }
                end
                v.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                v.Reflectance = 0
                v.CastShadow = false
            end
        end
    else
        light.GlobalShadows = fpsLightingCache.GS or true
        light.FogEnd = fpsLightingCache.FE or 100000
        for part, props in pairs(fpsCache) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                part.Material, part.Reflectance, part.CastShadow = props.M, props.R, props.C
            end
        end
        fpsCache = {}
    end
end
addcmd("fpsboost", {"fps"}, function(args, speaker)
    setFpsBoost(not fpsBoostOn)
    notify("FPS Boost", fpsBoostOn and "On" or "Off")
end)

local unjumpOn = false
local jumpLoop = nil
local function setUnlockJump(state)
    unjumpOn = state
    if state then
        if jumpLoop then jumpLoop:Disconnect() end
        jumpLoop = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then
                if h.JumpPower < 50 then h.JumpPower = 50 end
                if h.JumpHeight < 7.2 then h.JumpHeight = 7.2 end
                h.UseJumpPower = true
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
            end
        end)
    else
        if jumpLoop then jumpLoop:Disconnect(); jumpLoop = nil end
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            h.JumpPower = 0
            h.JumpHeight = 0
            h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
        end
    end
end
addcmd("unjump", {"unlockjump"}, function(args, speaker)
    setUnlockJump(not unjumpOn)
    notify("Unlock Jump", unjumpOn and "On" or "Off")
end)

addcmd("fling", {}, function(args, speaker)
    loadRemoteScript("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/K1LAS1K/Ultimate-Fling-GUI/main/flingscript.lua")
end)

addcmd("superring", {}, function(args, speaker)
    loadRemoteScript("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Lukashub-coder/Super-ring-V5/refs/heads/main/By%20lukas!!")
end)

addcmd("decal", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local id = 71391195558057
    local function spam(v)
        if v:IsA("Part") then
            for i = 0, 5 do
                local d = Instance.new("Decal")
                d.Face = i
                d.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=" .. id
                d.Parent = v
            end
        elseif v:IsA("Model") then
            for _, c in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do spam(c) end
        end
    end
    for _, v in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do spam(v) end
end)

addcmd("hint", {}, function(args, speaker)
    local text = table.concat(args, " ")
    local h = Instance.new("Hint")
    h.Parent = workspace
    h.Text = text ~= "" and text or "TEAM TUBERS93: join team today!!!!"
    task.wait(5)
    if h.Parent then h:Destroy() end
end)

local musicSound = nil
local function stopMusic()
    if musicSound and musicSound.Parent then
        musicSound:Destroy()
        musicSound = nil
    end
end
addcmd("music", {}, function(args, speaker)
    if musicSound and musicSound.Parent then
        stopMusic()
        return
    end
    local s = Instance.new("Sound")
    s.Looped = true
    s.Playing = true
    s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://103215672097028"
    s.Parent = workspace
    musicSound = s
end)
addcmd("stopmusic", {}, function(args, speaker)
    stopMusic()
end)

-- ============================================================
-- JERK (the classic, with a hub toggle)
-- ============================================================
local jerkActive = false
local jerkTool = nil
local jerkLoop = nil

local function startJerk()
    if jerkActive then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
    local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Backpack")
    if not (humanoid and backpack) then
        notify("Jerk", "No character yet")
        return
    end
    jerkActive = true

    local tool = Instance.new("Tool")
    tool.Name = "Jerk Off"
    tool.ToolTip = "in the stripped club. straight up \"jorking it\" . and by \"it\" , haha, well. let's justr say. My peanits."
    tool.RequiresHandle = false
    tool.Parent = backpack
    jerkTool = tool

    local jorkin = false
    local track = nil

    local function stopTomfoolery()
        jorkin = false
        if track then
            track:Stop()
            track = nil
        end
    end

    tool.Equipped:Connect(function() jorkin = true end)
    tool.Unequipped:Connect(stopTomfoolery)
    humanoid.Died:Connect(stopTomfoolery)

    jerkLoop = task.spawn(function()
        while jerkActive and task.wait() do
            if not jorkin then continue end
            local isR15 = humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
            if not track then
                local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                anim.AnimationId = not isR15 and "rbxassetid://72042024" or "rbxassetid://698251653"
                track = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
            end
            track:Play()
            track:AdjustSpeed(isR15 and 0.7 or 0.65)
            track.TimePosition = 0.6
            task.wait(0.1)
            while track and track.TimePosition < (not isR15 and 0.65 or 0.7) do task.wait(0.1) end
            if track then
                track:Stop()
                track = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function stopJerk()
    jerkActive = false
    if jerkLoop then
        task.cancel(jerkLoop)
        jerkLoop = nil
    end
    if jerkTool then
        jerkTool:Destroy()
        jerkTool = nil
    end
end

addcmd("jerk", {}, function(args, speaker)
    startJerk()
end)
addcmd("unjerk", {}, function(args, speaker)
    stopJerk()
end)

-- Auto re-run jerk on the new character after death while it is still active.
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if jerkActive then
        stopJerk()
        task.wait()
        startJerk()
    end
end)

local invisDragging = false
local function invisUpdateFromMouse()
    local abs = InvisSliderTrack.AbsolutePosition
    local w = InvisSliderTrack.AbsoluteSize.X
    if w <= 0 then return end
    local frac = (UserInputService:GetMouseLocation().X - abs.X) / w
    invisApplyValue(INVIS_SLIDER_MIN + (INVIS_SLIDER_MAX - INVIS_SLIDER_MIN) * math.clamp(frac, 0, 1), true)
end
InvisSliderTrack.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        invisUpdateFromMouse()
        invisDragging = true
    end
end)
InvisKnob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        invisDragging = true
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if invisDragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
        invisUpdateFromMouse()
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then invisDragging = false end
end)
for i, opt in ipairs(INVIS_OPTIONS) do
    local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.2, -7, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.2, (i == 1) and 5 or 2, 0, 32),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn, Text = tostring(opt.value), TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 10, Parent = InvisSection,
    })
    round(b, 5)
    stroke(b, COLOR.border, 1)
    bindFont(b, "bold")
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        invisSetDepth(opt.value)
        showToast("Invis Offset: " .. opt.value, "on")
    end)
end

HUB.collapsibleHeader(CmdPage, "COMMANDS", 36)
newInstance("TextLabel", {
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 120), Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 52),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
    TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
    Text = "fly/unfly, noclip/clip, swim/unswim, speed, jumppower, god, freecam/unfreecam, fov, antiafk, goto, tweengoto, tppos, offset, respawn, jerk/unjerk, notify, help. Mods: invisible, fling, fpsboost, unjump, superring, decal, hint, music, stopmusic. Prefix with ; or type in the bar above.",
    TextColor3 = COLOR.textDim, Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 11, Parent = CmdPage,
})
-- REDESIGN [SMART REFLOW]: command help list bottom.
HUB.contentBottom[CmdPage] = 172

local cmdHistory = {}
local historyCount = 0
local function runCommand()
    local text = CmdBar.Text
    if text == "" then return end
    table.insert(cmdHistory, 1, text)
    if #cmdHistory > 30 then table.remove(cmdHistory) end
    historyCount = 0
    CmdBar.Text = ""
    execCmd(text, LocalPlayer)
end
CmdRunBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(runCommand)
CmdBar.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
    if enterPressed then runCommand() end
end)
CmdBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
        historyCount = math.min(historyCount + 1, #cmdHistory)
        if cmdHistory[historyCount] then CmdBar.Text = cmdHistory[historyCount] end
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
        historyCount = math.max(historyCount - 1, 0)
        CmdBar.Text = cmdHistory[historyCount] or ""
    end
end)

return CmdPage
end
runCommandEngine()
end
-- ============================================================
-- PART F5. FLING MENU + INVENTORY (multi-target fling rebuilt
-- into the Schiz UI, plus the pocket inventory). Mirrors the
-- original command-engine scope exactly: rows live on the Server
-- view, floating panels parent to the hub ScreenGui, and
-- setRowStatus (do-scope) drives the row labels.
-- ============================================================
local FlingTargets = {}
local FlingCheckmarks = {}
local FlingActive = false
local FlingMenuFrame = nil
local FlingListScroll = nil
local FlingStatusLabel = nil
local FlingLoop = nil

local function FlingCountSelected()
    local count = 0
    for _ in pairs(FlingTargets) do count = count + 1 end
    return count
end

local function FlingUpdateStatus()
    if not FlingStatusLabel then return end
    local count = FlingCountSelected()
    FlingStatusLabel.Text = FlingActive and ("Flinging " .. count .. " target(s)") or (count .. " target(s) selected")
    FlingStatusLabel.TextColor3 = FlingActive and COLOR.warn or COLOR.text
end

local function FlingNotify(title, text)
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = title, Text = text, Duration = 2 })
    end)
end

local function FlingToggleAll(select)
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            if select then
                FlingTargets[player.Name] = player
            else
                FlingTargets[player.Name] = nil
            end
            local c = FlingCheckmarks[player.Name]
            if c then c.Visible = FlingTargets[player.Name] ~= nil end
        end
    end
    FlingUpdateStatus()
end

local function FlingRefreshPlayerList()
    if not FlingListScroll then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(FlingListScroll:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    FlingCheckmarks = {}
    local list = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(list, p) end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower() end)
    for i, player in ipairs(list) do
        local sel = FlingTargets[player.Name] ~= nil
        local row = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 26), LayoutOrder = i,
            BackgroundColor3 = sel and COLOR.accent or COLOR.playerBtn,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = FlingListScroll,
        })
        round(row, 5)
        local mark = newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = sel and "\xE2\x9C\x93" or "", TextColor3 = COLOR.on,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 14, Parent = row,
        })
        newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = player.Name, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
            Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Parent = row,
        })
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if FlingTargets[player.Name] then
                FlingTargets[player.Name] = nil
                row.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.playerBtn
                mark.Text = ""
            else
                FlingTargets[player.Name] = player
                row.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent
                mark.Text = "\xE2\x9C\x93"
            end
            FlingUpdateStatus()
        end)
        FlingCheckmarks[player.Name] = mark
    end
    FlingListScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #list * 30 + 4)
end

local function FlingTargetPlayer(TargetPlayer)
    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local RootPart = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
    local TCharacter = TargetPlayer.Character
    if not TCharacter then return end

    local THumanoid, TRootPart, THead, Accessory, Handle
    if TCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then THumanoid = TCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") end
    if THumanoid and THumanoid.RootPart then TRootPart = THumanoid.RootPart end
    if TCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head") then THead = TCharacter.Head end
    if TCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory") then Accessory = TCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory") end
    if Accessory and Accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle") then Handle = Accessory.Handle end

    if Character and Humanoid and RootPart then
        if RootPart.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 then getgenv().OldPos = RootPart.CFrame end

        if THumanoid and THumanoid.Sit then return FlingNotify("Error", TargetPlayer.Name .. " is sitting") end

        if THead then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = THead
        elseif Handle then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Handle
        elseif THumanoid and TRootPart then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = THumanoid
        end

        if not TCharacter:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") then return end

        local FPos = function(BasePart, Pos, Ang)
            RootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(BasePart.Position) * Pos * Ang
            Character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(BasePart.Position) * Pos * Ang)
            RootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(9e7, 9e7 * 10, 9e7)
            RootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(9e8, 9e8, 9e8)
        end

        local SFBasePart = function(BasePart)
            local TimeToWait = 2
            local Time = tick()
            local Angle = 0
            repeat
                if RootPart and THumanoid then
                    if BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 then
                        Angle = Angle + 100
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection * BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection * BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection * BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection * BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                    else
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, 1.5, THumanoid.WalkSpeed), CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, -THumanoid.WalkSpeed), CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, 1.5, THumanoid.WalkSpeed), CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0), CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0), CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0), CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                        FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0), CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0))
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
            until Time + TimeToWait < tick() or not FlingActive
        end

        -- [V6] Save the real fall height before NaN-ing it, so the
        -- fling restores it instead of leaving the workspace broken.
        getgenv().FPDH = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0 / 0

        local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        BV.Parent = RootPart
        BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)

        Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)

        if TRootPart then
            SFBasePart(TRootPart)
        elseif THead then
            SFBasePart(THead)
        elseif Handle then
            SFBasePart(Handle)
        else
            return FlingNotify("Error", TargetPlayer.Name .. " has no valid parts")
        end

        BV:Destroy()
        Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Humanoid

        if getgenv().OldPos then
            repeat
                RootPart.CFrame = getgenv().OldPos * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
                Character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(getgenv().OldPos * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0))
                Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                for _, part in pairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.Velocity, part.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(), Vector3.new()
                    end
                end
                task.wait()
            until (RootPart.Position - getgenv().OldPos.p).Magnitude < 25
            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = getgenv().FPDH
        end
    else
        return FlingNotify("Error", "Your character is not ready")
    end
end

local function FlingStart()
    if FlingActive then return end
    local count = FlingCountSelected()
    if count == 0 then
        if FlingStatusLabel then FlingStatusLabel.Text = "No targets selected!" end
        return
    end
    FlingActive = true
    FlingUpdateStatus()
    FlingLoop = task.spawn(function()
        while FlingActive do
            local validTargets = {}
            for name, player in pairs(FlingTargets) do
                if player and player.Parent then
                    validTargets[name] = player
                else
                    FlingTargets[name] = nil
                    local c = FlingCheckmarks[name]
                    if c then c.Visible = false end
                end
            end
            for _, player in pairs(validTargets) do
                if FlingActive then
                    pcall(FlingTargetPlayer, player)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                else
                    break
                end
            end
            FlingUpdateStatus()
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

local function FlingStop()
    FlingActive = false
    FlingUpdateStatus()
end

local function FlingClose()
    FlingStop()
    if FlingMenuFrame then
        FlingMenuFrame:Destroy()
        FlingMenuFrame = nil
    end
    FlingListScroll, FlingStatusLabel = nil, nil
    FlingCheckmarks = {}
end

local function FlingOpen()
    if FlingMenuFrame then return end
    FlingMenuFrame = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 330), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -120, 0.5, -165),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.bg, Active = true, Parent = HUB.screenGui,
    })
    round(FlingMenuFrame, 12)
    stroke(FlingMenuFrame, COLOR.border, 1)
    local hdr = newInstance("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Parent = FlingMenuFrame })
    round(hdr, 12)
    newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Text = "Multi-Fling", Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Parent = hdr,
    })
    local closeBtn = newInstance("TextButton", {
        Text = "X", Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, -11),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.warn, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12, Parent = hdr,
    })
    round(closeBtn, 5)
    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(FlingClose)
    FlingStatusLabel = newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Text = "0 target(s) selected", Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 40),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
        Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 11,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Parent = FlingMenuFrame,
    })
    local cont = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 176), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 62),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = FlingMenuFrame,
    })
    FlingListScroll = newInstance("ScrollingFrame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel,
        ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = COLOR.textDim,
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Parent = cont,
    })
    round(FlingListScroll, 6)
    stroke(FlingListScroll, COLOR.border, 1)
    newInstance("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = FlingListScroll })
    -- [v3] The original stacked all four buttons at y=244 (SELECT ALL was
    -- covered by START FLING); split into a proper 2x2 grid (244 / 276).
    local function flingBtn(col, row, text, color, cb)
        local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(col, 6, 0, 244 + row * 32),
            BackgroundColor3 = color, Text = text, TextColor3 = COLOR.text,
            Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 11, Parent = FlingMenuFrame,
        })
        round(b, 6)
        bindFont(b, "bold")
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
        return b
    end
    flingBtn(0, 0, "SELECT ALL", COLOR.panel, function() FlingToggleAll(true) end)
    flingBtn(0.5, 0, "DESELECT", COLOR.panel, function() FlingToggleAll(false) end)
    flingBtn(0, 1, "START FLING", COLOR.on, FlingStart)
    flingBtn(0.5, 1, "STOP", COLOR.warn, FlingStop)
    FlingRefreshPlayerList()
    FlingUpdateStatus()
    if BUILD_VARIANT == "glass" then recolorTree() end
end

serverSection("EXTRAS")
local FlingStatus, FlingSet, FlingGet, FlingCB = serverRow("FLING", nil)
FlingCB(function(on)
    if on then
        FlingOpen()
    else
        FlingClose()
    end
    setRowStatus(FlingStatus, on)
end)
HUB.rows.server.fling = { status = FlingStatus, set = FlingSet, get = FlingGet, cb = FlingCB }

-- ============================================================
-- POCKET INVENTORY (own floating panel, disconnect-safe)
-- ============================================================
local InvFrame, InvScroll = nil, nil
local InvConnections = {}
local function UpdateInvList()
    if not InvFrame or not InvScroll then return end
    for _, c in ipairs(InvScroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end end
    local list = {}
    local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if bp then for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(list, t) end end end
    if char then for _, t in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(list, t) end end end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.Name < b.Name end)
    for i, t in ipairs(list) do
        local eq = char and t.Parent == char
        local b = newInstance("TextButton", {
            LayoutOrder = i, Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32),
            BackgroundColor3 = eq and COLOR.playerBtn or COLOR.panel,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = InvScroll,
        })
        round(b, 6)
        newInstance("TextLabel", {
            Text = t.Name, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = currentFontPreset.regular, TextSize = 11,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextColor3 = eq and COLOR.text or COLOR.textDim, Parent = b,
        })
        if eq then
            local ind = newInstance("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.6, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.2, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.accent, Parent = b,
            })
            round(ind, 2)
        end
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local cc = LocalPlayer.Character
            if cc and cc:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                if eq then cc.Humanoid:UnequipTools() else if t and t.Parent then cc.Humanoid:EquipTool(t) end end
            end
        end)
    end
end
local function SetupInvConnections()
    for _, c in ipairs(InvConnections) do c:Disconnect() end
    InvConnections = {}
    if not InvFrame then return end
    local bp, char = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), LocalPlayer.Character
    if bp then
        table.insert(InvConnections, bp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c) if c:IsA("Tool") then UpdateInvList() end end))
        table.insert(InvConnections, bp.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(c) if c:IsA("Tool") then UpdateInvList() end end))
    end
    if char then
        table.insert(InvConnections, char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c) if c:IsA("Tool") then UpdateInvList() end end))
        table.insert(InvConnections, char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(c) if c:IsA("Tool") then UpdateInvList() end end))
    end
end
local function ToggleInv(open)
    if not open then
        if InvFrame then InvFrame:Destroy(); InvFrame = nil end
        for _, c in ipairs(InvConnections) do c:Disconnect() end
        InvConnections = {}
        return
    end
    InvFrame = newInstance("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 155, 0, 185), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 90, 0.5, -92),
        BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.bg, Active = true, Parent = HUB.screenGui,
    })
    round(InvFrame, 12)
    stroke(InvFrame, COLOR.border, 1)
    local hdr = newInstance("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), BackgroundColor3 = COLOR.panel, Parent = InvFrame })
    round(hdr, 12)
    newInstance("TextLabel", {
        Text = "Inventory", Font = currentFontPreset.bold, TextSize = 12,
        TextColor3 = COLOR.text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Parent = hdr,
    })
    local cont = newInstance("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -40), Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 37), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = InvFrame })
    InvScroll = newInstance("ScrollingFrame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScrollBarThickness = 2,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Parent = cont,
    })
    newInstance("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = InvScroll })
    SetupInvConnections()
    UpdateInvList()
    if BUILD_VARIANT == "glass" then recolorTree() end
end

local InvStatus, InvSet, InvGet, InvCB = serverRow("POCKET INV", nil)
InvCB(function(on)
    setRowStatus(InvStatus, on)
    ToggleInv(on)
end)
HUB.rows.server.inv = { status = InvStatus, set = InvSet, get = InvGet, cb = InvCB }
